# Priority Buyers And Importers For Garlic

Prepared for Unyra Group LLC on 2026-04-19.

## How to use this appendix

- Source: public buyer-directory and customs-summary pages, primarily Trademo, captured on 2026-04-15, 2026-04-16, 2026-04-18, 2026-04-19, and 2026-04-24; Veritrade and ImportGenius are used only where explicitly noted.
- Metric: `Shipment Count` as displayed by the source, or public shipment evidence where a count table is not available.
- Interpretation: this is best used for prospecting priority and lead generation, not as final credit/risk underwriting.
- Signal quality:
  - `High`: page keyword is `fresh-garlic` or `peeled-garlic`.
  - `Medium`: page keyword is generic `garlic` and may mix adjacent garlic product descriptions.

## Fresh Whole, Peeled, Frozen And Processed Garlic Signal - Sweden

Source: [WITS Sweden HS 070320 imports 2023](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/SWE/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/ALL/product/070320), [Grönsaksmästarna garlic](https://www.gronsaksmastarna.se/en/prouducts/onions/garlic/), [Volza Sweden garlic HSN 21039090 buyers](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic/buyers/buyers-in-sweden/hsn-code-21039090/), [IND Traders wholesale catalog](https://indtradersab.com/), [Trademo IND Traders profile](https://www.trademo.com/companies/ind-traders-ab/46693756), [Menigo frozen garlic category](https://www.menigo.se/produkter/mat/frysta-skordeprodukter/frysta-gronsaker/lok/vitlok), [Wernsing Food Solutions about](https://www.wernsing.se/english-about-us), [GG Handel contact](https://gghandel.se/kontakt/), [Saigon Food contact](https://www.saigon.se/saigon-food-kontakt/), [Volza Sweden garlic sauce buyers](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic-sauce/buyers/buyers-in-sweden/), [Matkompaniet garlic category](https://www.matkompaniet.se/en/catalog/node/smaksattare-vitlok), [Matkompaniet contact](https://www.matkompaniet.se/sv/content/kontakta-oss), [Werners contact](https://wgs.se/kontakta-oss), [Mathem Risberg crushed garlic](https://www.mathem.se/se/products/8740-risberg-vitlok-krossad/), [Aayath contact](https://aayath.com/contact), and [Taam Food Group official](https://taamfoodgroup.se/vara-bolag/)
Signal quality: High for country-level fresh/chilled HS 070320 demand and official fresh, peeled and frozen garlic-format evidence; medium-high for processed garlic, sauce, paste and ethnic-grocery buyer tables because the free public shipment pages are mostly HSN 21039090 or garlic-sauce rather than named fresh-whole HS 070320 import ownership. Paid customs should separate fresh whole garlic from paste, sauce, dehydrated, frozen/IQF and peeled formats before FCL quoting.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Grönsaksmästarna AB | Strongest contact-ready official fresh-whole route. Official product pages list garlic as a standard item with import availability, and WITS confirms Sweden imported about USD 18.18M and 4.15M kg of fresh/chilled garlic in 2023. |
| 2 | Dole Nordic / Everfresh / Ewerman / FG Centralen / Hebe / Svensk FoodService | Scaled fresh-produce importer/distributor layer. These official sources show national or regional fruit-and-vegetable import, wholesale, logistics and foodservice infrastructure, but most still need direct garlic-shipment validation. |
| 3 | Menigo / Martin & Servera / Grönsakshallen / Outofhome | Best official peeled, frozen and foodservice garlic demand layer. Menigo shows frozen garlic/IQF-style category evidence, Martin & Servera lists peeled garlic, Grönsakshallen lists 5 kg organic garlic and Outofhome lists foodservice garlic formats. |
| 4 | GG Handel i Skara / Wernsing Food Solutions | Best direct frozen-garlic supplier validation found in this pass. Menigo identifies GG Handel as supplier for frozen garlic items, while Wernsing/GG official pages validate ambient, chilled and frozen import/distribution to wholesalers, food producers, public sector and grocery retail. |
| 5 | IND Traders / Saigon Food / Matkompaniet / Hot Chilly-Taam / Aayath | Processed-garlic and ethnic grocery import layer. Public Volza and Trademo pages expose IND Traders, Saigon Food, Matkompaniet and Hot Chilly garlic paste, garlic sauce or HSN 21039090 shipment signals, while official pages validate importer/distributor channels and contacts. |
| 6 | Werners Gourmetservice / Risberg / Santa Maria / Torebrings / Snabbgross | Dehydrated, crushed, paste, spice and premium ingredient layer. Useful for processed-garlic sales and referral intelligence, but lower priority than fresh whole, peeled refrigerated and frozen/IQF routes for container-level outreach. |
| 7 | Dagab / ICA / Snabbgross / Svensk Cater / Trädgårdshallen / Leda / Mälardalens | National grocery, cash-and-carry, foodservice and regional produce distribution routes. These are useful demand validators and possible referrals, but public evidence is mostly broad produce or retail demand rather than direct fresh-garlic import ownership. |

## Fresh Whole, Peeled And Foodservice Garlic Signal - Finland

Source: [Valkoliekki HORECA](https://www.valkoliekki.fi/horeca/?lang=en), [Kespro peeled garlic](https://www.kespro.com/tuotteet/menu-valkosipulinkynsi-kuorittu-1kg-6408641079951), [Kespro frozen chopped garlic](https://www.kespro.com/tuotteet/menu-valkosipuli-hienonnettu-250g-irtopakaste-6410405224033), [Wihuri Metro-tukku peeled garlic](https://www.metrotukku.fi/en/EUR/products/fresh-fruits-and-vegetables/garlic-peeled-1kg-es/6430035160013), [Elatukku frozen peeled garlic](https://www.elatukku.fi/pakasteet/asian-choice-garlic-peeled-kuorittu-valkosipuli-1kg/p/8578/), [Valio Aimo peeled garlic](https://www.valioaimo.fi/toimitustukku/valkosipuli-kuorittu-1-kg-kiina/), [Valio Aimo contact and procurement page](https://www.valioaimo.fi/en/contact-information/), [Satotukku official](https://satotukku.fi/en), [S-kaupat fresh garlic / Satotukku](https://www.s-kaupat.fi/tuote/valkosipuli-tuore/2005777200002), [Garlica / Kespro peeled garlic](https://www.kespro.com/tuotteet/menu-valkosipulinkynsi-kuorittu-1kg-6408641079951), [Apetit professional frozen foodservice SKU](https://apetit.fi/ammattilaiset/tuotteet/apetit-kasvis-jalapenonugget-4-kg), and [Fresh Servant fresh garlic SKU](https://www.s-kaupat.fi/tuote/fresh-servant-pme-valkosipuli-100-g/6400651029551)
Signal quality: High for official peeled-garlic, frozen chopped garlic, self-imported garlic and national foodservice catalog evidence; medium for processor rows where garlic is an ingredient rather than the headline imported SKU. Paid customs should separate HS 070320 fresh whole garlic from peeled, frozen, value-added and retail private-label flows before FCL quoting.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Valkoliekki Oy | Strongest direct garlic processor/importer route. Official HORECA pages say Valkoliekki imports Spanish garlic itself, machine-peels cloves, supplies whole peeled cloves plus fresh and frozen packs to Finland's largest wholesalers and food-industry buyers, and refines about 200,000 kg of garlic annually. |
| 2 | Kespro / Wihuri Metro-tukku / Cemet Oy-Elatukku | Best national foodservice and wholesale peeled/frozen routes. Kespro lists Menu peeled garlic and frozen chopped garlic; Wihuri Metro-tukku lists Garlica peeled Spanish garlic as an order product; Elatukku lists frozen peeled garlic and identifies Cemet Oy / Elatukku as importer. |
| 3 | Valio Aimo / Meira Nova | National HoReCa delivery-wholesale scale. Valio Aimo lists peeled garlic 1kg China and publishes procurement contacts for fruits and vegetables; Meira Nova is named by Valkoliekki as a garlic private-label customer and publishes national foodservice contacts. |
| 4 | Satotukku Oy | Best fresh whole-garlic importer/produce-wholesaler challenger after Valkoliekki. Official pages describe one of Finland's largest independent fruit-and-vegetable wholesalers, imported vegetable sourcing from around 15 countries and national cold-chain distribution, while S-kaupat product data links Satotukku to a fresh-garlic SKU from Egypt. |
| 5 | Garlica Oy | Strong peeled-garlic supplier/processor validation through Kespro and Wihuri Metro-tukku product pages for peeled Spanish garlic 1kg. Public business-directory records provide direct Järvenpää contact details. |
| 6 | Apetit Oyj / Apetit Ruoka Oy | Large frozen-food and foodservice processor route where garlic is an ingredient input. Official professional product pages list garlic-containing frozen SKUs and central wholesaler codes for Wihuri, Meira Nova, Heino, Patu and Kespro. |
| 7 | Sevan / Jaakkolan Tukku / SOK / Kesko K-Ruoka / Fresh Servant | Contact-ready garlic demand layer across crushed garlic, fresh produce wholesale, frozen chopped garlic, fresh retail garlic and ready-meal ingredient use. These are credible buyers but need paid-data or supplier conversations before assuming full-container fresh whole ownership. |
| 8 | Herkkumaa / Fine Foods / Pure Garden / Kähmän Kaali / Hinjärv / Eclipse / Siili Food / Lidl / Tokmanni / Minimani / HalpaHalli | Lower-priority processed, grower, retail or value-added routes. Useful for peeled, paste, sauce, black-garlic or retail SKU intelligence, but should not outrank the national foodservice, produce-wholesale and processor/importer layer for container-level outreach. |

## Fresh Garlic, Foodservice And Allium Buyer Signal - Panama

Source: [Volza - Panama buyers / garlic or Indian potatoes, HSN 0703](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic-or-indian-potatoes/buyers/buyers-in-panama/hsn-code-0703/), [Sysco Panama official garlic SKU](https://www.syscopanama.com/ajo-picado-en-agua-badia-132-o-pa176640.html), [Dicarina official services](https://dicarina.com/servicios), [Frulesa official store](https://frulesa.com.pa/tienda/), [Vegetales Fresquita peeled garlic](https://fresquitacr.com/producto/ajo-pelado-1-kg-2), [Agrochef official garlic page](https://agrochef.com/products/ajo-en-tiras-uni), [Freskura official garlic page](https://www.freskura.com.pa/product-page/ajo), [Distribuidora CTC official boxed garlic](https://distribuidoractcpanama.com/producto/ajo-blanco-empacada-por-caja/), [Ventas de Frutas y Legumbres MAY official](https://www.ventasfrutaslegumbresmay.com/), and [La Ferduleria official store](https://ferduleria.com/tienda/)
Signal quality: High for Volza HSN 0703 fresh-garlic/allium buyer names and official garlic distributor/foodservice SKU pages; medium for allium entries such as garlic stems and for public-customs rows without official procurement contacts.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Grupo Agrocampo S.A. | Volza's Panama HSN 0703 garlic/allium table ranks Grupo Agrocampo first with 54 shipments and visible fresh-garlic/peeled-garlic entries; TradeImex also exposes a Panama HS 070320 garlic-sprout sample. No official procurement contact was verified. |
| 2 | Westlake Onions Sales S.A. / Import Agrocampo PMA S.A. | Volza lists Westlake with 42 shipments and visible 29,000 kg/3,177 kg fresh-garlic entries, and Import Agrocampo with 39 garlic/allium shipments plus visible fresh and fresh peeled garlic entries. Both are paid-customs ownership targets before direct outreach. |
| 3 | Importadora y Exportadora Hermanos Gago / Importadora Grupo Limite PA / Kingmax | Hermanos Gago adds customs-backed fresh-garlic evidence plus public company-directory import/export distribution context. Grupo Limite has visible 3,150-bag fresh-garlic entries. Kingmax is high-count HSN 0703 allium evidence but visible entries are garlic stem/chives, so verify bulb-garlic ownership before quoting. |
| 4 | Sysco Panama / Dicarina | Strongest contact-ready national foodservice and cold-chain distribution routes. Sysco directly lists minced garlic SKUs and serves 2,500 businesses; Dicarina validates national distributor scale, foodservice coverage, cold chain and container-processing capacity but does not publish a direct garlic SKU. |
| 5 | Frulesa / Vegetales Fresquita / Agrochef / Freskura / Distribuidora CTC / MAY | Best official fresh, peeled or boxed garlic distributor/wholesale routes with public contacts, Merca Panama or refrigerated-produce positioning and business/restaurant/hotel/institutional supply language. |
| 6 | La Ferduleria / Cerro Punta / Riba Smith / Superunico / PriceSmart / Super 99 | Useful contactable retail or smaller distributor SKU demand, but lower FCL priority than customs-backed importers and wholesaler/foodservice channels. |
| 7 | Ojo de Agua / Don Don / Feax / El Machetazo / Romero / Aliverys | Format or channel validation for garlic powder, seasoning, fresh produce delivery and supermarket demand; useful for processed-garlic follow-up rather than first fresh-whole FCL outreach. |

## Fresh Whole, Peeled And Foodservice Garlic Signal - Portugal

Source: [Rodrigues de Paula official](https://www.rodrigues-de-paula.pt/en_gb/index), [Batopes official](https://www.batopes.pt/), [Batatas Mirense garlic page](https://www.batatasmirense.pt/produtos/alhos/), [Manuel Patrício official](https://manuelpatricio.pt/), [Os Saleiros official](https://www.saleiros.pt/index.php/sobre-nos/quem-somos), [As Oliveirinhas official](https://www.oliveirinhas.com/), [Batatas Capela official](https://www.batatascapela.pt/page/95/quem_somos), [Giants official](https://giants.pt/), [Luís Vicente official contacts](https://luisvicente.com/en/contactos/), [LUSOBATATA official products](https://www.lusobatata.com/produtos/), and [Sogenave peeled garlic SKU](https://lojaonline.sogenave.pt/categorias/frutas-e-legumes/legumes/horticolas-frescos-preparados/alho-fresco-descascado-1kg/)
Signal quality: High for direct official fresh/packed garlic, import/export and peeled-garlic evidence. Luís Vicente is a scale-channel lead supported by official produce-distribution pages and a public Azores garlic-import table, but paid customs should validate direct HS 070320 ownership before FCL quoting.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Rodrigues de Paula, Lda. | Official pages describe about 50 years commercializing garlic, national production plus import/export activity, purple garlic from Portugal/Spain, white garlic, peeled garlic and 10 kg/6 kg formats with direct Coimbra contacts. |
| 2 | Batopes - Batatas Lopes | Official pages validate packed garlic, imported supply complements from Spain, France, Germany, Belgium and Holland, packing lines, cold storage and owned transport, making it one of Portugal's best full-container-style fresh garlic routes. |
| 3 | Batatas Mirense, Lda. | Official garlic page lists packed and bulk garlic, daily garlic tonnage, large storage and production capacity, pack sizes from 250 g to 25 kg, IFS Food/ISO 9001 references and public contacts. |
| 4 | Manuel Patrício / Os Saleiros / As Oliveirinhas / Batatas Capela | These official pages validate garlic inside potatoes/onions/vegetable wholesale, packing, import/export, distribution or African/island export routes. They are strong paid-customs reconciliation targets for HS 070320 ownership. |
| 5 | Giants / Luís Vicente | Giants directly lists Alho, Cebola e Gengibre and import/export coverage across 22 countries; Luís Vicente validates a 50,000+ tonne produce-distribution platform and a public Azores garlic-import signal. Use paid customs to confirm garlic-specific procurement. |
| 6 | LUSOBATATA / Batcel / Sogenave / Primovegetal | Best contact-ready peeled refrigerated garlic routes, including IV Gama peeling, foodservice distribution, processing-factory supply and professional catalog evidence. |
| 7 | Aviludo / Humberto Marques / makro Portugal / Frimário / MERCACHEFE | Foodservice and wholesale routes for frozen chopped garlic, peeled garlic or frozen garlic, useful when selling frozen/IQF or prepared formats rather than fresh whole containers. |
| 8 | Retail and specialty rows | Frutas Martina, A.M. Frutas, Nfrutas, Jóia do Campo, Gourmet Italiano, Auchan, Supermercado Lavrador, El Corte Inglés, Continente and Espiga validate SKU demand but should not outrank importer/packer/wholesaler routes for FCL fresh garlic outreach. |

## Fresh Garlic And Allium Buyer Signal - Taiwan, China

Source: [Marland official services page](https://www.marland.tw/services.html), [Volza - Taiwan buyers / garlic or chutney](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic-or-chutney/buyers/buyers-in-taiwan/), [Volza - Taiwan import / fresh garlic](https://www.volza.com/p/fresh-garlic/import/import-in-taiwan/), [Man Ku Taiwan company registry](https://twbi.com.tw/company/1455), [Tian Yi Feng Trademo company profile](https://www.trademo.com/companies/tian-yi-feng-enterprise-co-ltd/35775720), [Roots official garlic page](https://www.rootscomp.net/zh/products/garlic), [Fuli official](https://www.fuligarlic.com/), [Sun Fresh official garlic-cloves page](https://www.sun-fresh.com.tw/product/%E7%B2%BE%E9%81%B8%E8%92%9C%E4%BB%81/), [Point Trading IEAT profile](https://www.ieatpe.org.tw/tketc/company_data.asp?company_no=214), and [Volza - Taiwan buyers / garlic paste onion](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic-paste-or-onion/buyers/buyers-in-taiwan/)
Signal quality: High for Marland official fresh-garlic import evidence and Tian Yi Feng HS 070320 fresh-garlic records; medium for allium-heavy public customs tables where visible records mix onion/cabbage with garlic keywords. Paid customs should reconcile HS 070320 ownership before quoting FCL volume.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Marland Trade Co., Ltd. / Marland Trading Corp. | Official pages validate fresh garlic imports into Taiwan from Egypt, Spain and Argentina, year-round supply to processing factories, restaurant-chain and food-factory custom packing, and wholesale-market distribution; Volza also lists Marland in Taiwan garlic-category buyer data. |
| 2 | Man Ku Cold Storage Co., Ltd. / 滿固農業生物科技股份有限公司 | Volza lists Man Ku among Taiwan garlic/chutney buyers and shows HSN 0703 import exposure from Spain with Ajos Garvaz as a supplier, while Taiwan registry data validates import/export eligibility, phone, representative and Yunlin address. |
| 3 | Roots Company | Official Roots pages describe Taiwan-dedicated garlic production and 40-50 containers per season, making it a strong source/channel route even though it may be more competitor/supplier than independent importer-buyer. |
| 4 | Tian Yi Feng Enterprise Co., Ltd. | Trademo lists HS 070320 at 100% of visible import value with multiple 25,925 kg fresh-garlic bulb entries, and Volza profile data lists garlic/granulated garlic among primary imports; no official procurement contact was verified. |
| 5 | Fuli Agricultural Enterprise / Sun Fresh Agricultural Produce | Fuli is a contact-ready garlic raw-material wholesaler serving market vendors, restaurants and food processors with Taiwanese, imported, clove, mince and paste formats; Sun Fresh is the strongest contact-ready peeled-garlic cold-chain route. |
| 6 | Point Trading / Sung Ya International / Tsann Lin | These are paid-customs allium-channel targets: Volza/Trademo public data show high garlic-paste/onion or HS 070310 allium shipment counts, but visible free examples skew onion/cabbage-heavy, so direct garlic volume needs paid validation before outreach. |
| 7 | LONGKAE / Fuang Fe / Taiwan Garlic Trading and black-garlic processors | LONGKAE validates Argentine garlic import language; Fuang Fe, TGT Fresh and other processor rows validate garlic chips, peeled black garlic, garlic sauces and value-added formats for format-specific sales paths. |

Source: [Volza - Mauritius buyers / garlic paste](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic-paste/buyers/buyers-in-mauritius/), [Volza - Mauritius buyers / pickle garlic](https://www.volza.com/p/pickle-garlic/buyers/buyers-in-mauritius/), [Volza - Mauritius buyers / garlic sauce](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic-sauce/buyers/buyers-in-mauritius/), and [Trademo - Mauritius buyers / garlic paste](https://www.trademo.com/mauritius/buyers/garlic-paste)
Signal quality: Medium to high for processed garlic demand; these are paste, pickle and sauce signals rather than fresh whole garlic import demand. Use AMB permit intelligence and private importer validation before any fresh-garlic FCL outreach.

| Rank | Buyer | Public shipment signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Relish Marketing Ltd | Volza's garlic-paste page identifies Relish as the dominant Mauritius garlic-paste buyer in the public summary, and Volza's pickle-garlic page also lists Relish among the top Mauritius pickle-garlic buyers. Signal quality: High for official-source garlic specialization and pan-European channel fit; medium for shipment evidence because no free public shipment table was added in this pass.
Interpretation note: Use Garlic Solutions as the European Union aggregate lead for fresh whole and peeled refrigerated garlic outreach. Use Greenyard, Ardo, Eosta, METRO, Transgourmet, Bonduelle, JERSA, Paris Ail, Marni, Exotimex, Rida, Bud Holland and Denimpex as channel-validation or paid-customs reconciliation routes before assigning broader EU FCL ownership.

| Rank | Buyer | Public Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Garlic Solutions | Official pages describe one of Europe's largest garlic-clove suppliers, more than four million cloves supplied weekly, supermarkets, fresh meal boxes and wholesale catering outlets, bulk bulbs, peeled and unpeeled cloves, black garlic, organic garlic, Spain-origin year-round varieties, peeled-garlic bulk and private-label options, FSSC 22000, Skal organic certification and direct Waddinxveen phone/email/address. |
| 2 | JERSA Fruchtimport GmbH | Official pages validate direct garlic import and wholesale in Germany since 1986, global sourcing and professional distribution. |
| 3 | Exotimex Europe BV | Official pages validate fruit-and-vegetable import/re-export plus China-origin peeled garlic in 4 x 2.5 kg year-round packaging. |
| 4 | Bud Holland | Official pages validate international exotic fruit/vegetable import plus fresh garlic, peeled garlic, garlic strands and black garlic for catering and specialty channels. |
| 5 | Denimpex | Official pages validate garlic imported from around the world plus peeled garlic for foodservice and processing customers. |
| 6 | Greenyard / Ardo / METRO / Transgourmet / Bonduelle | Broad EU distribution and processor bench for retail, foodservice, professional vegetable channels and frozen/IQF validation; garlic ownership must be checked in paid customs before FCL quoting. |

Official-source review 2026-05-03: Garlic Solutions is now normalized as European Union rank 1 in the main database and top-ranked file. Earlier EU rows ranked broad produce platforms above Garlic Solutions, but Garlic Solutions is a better Unyra buyer target because it directly handles garlic at scale and publishes the clearest fresh/peeled garlic format evidence. Paid customs should still confirm shipment ownership and supplier lanes before quoting containers.

Interpretation note: Use JN Riqueza first for Philippines fresh whole-garlic FCL ownership validation because it has the strongest combined Trademo fresh-garlic table, company-profile HS 070320 and Volza HS 0703209000 evidence. Use JLPM, Rochim, Krissam and RJJ Green Farm as the next paid-customs fresh whole targets. Use Hordex as the cleaner official-source outreach route for processed/dehydrated garlic ingredients and food-factory, repacker, dealer, caterer and supermarket channels.

Hordex moves to rank 6 and remains the strongest official contact-ready route for processed/dehydrated garlic ingredients because official pages validate a food-ingredient importer, supplier, distributor, wholesaler and retailer with nationwide reach, FDA License to Operate as food distributor/importer, Bureau of Customs importer accreditation, Manila and Cebu offices, multiple direct phone/Viber lines and China-origin garlic minced, granulated and powder SKUs in 25 kg bag formats. | 4 | STO / BHM / Equics / Hebron | STO is strategically important because public reports identify garlic among essential/staple food items; BHM shows 3400+ customers, 15 warehouses and refrigerated storage; Equics supplies fresh perishables and dry goods to resorts; Hebron has direct HS 070320 and Trademo garlic-buyer signals. |
| 5 | Redwave / AGORA-Bright Brothers / Fari / Euro Marketing | Retail and wholesale channels with large supermarket networks, food sourcing, fresh-goods packing/chilling/freezing, imported fruits/vegetables/foods and resort/foodservice distribution. Use as retail procurement validation unless paid data links them to direct garlic imports. |
| 6 | Maldives Inflight Catering | Institutional foodservice buyer with official 4000 meals/day capacity, ISO/HACCP/HALAL standards and continuous global ingredient sourcing; public trade pages show import/garlic-buyer signals, but procurement ownership needs validation. |

Source: [WITS - fresh/chilled garlic exports to Greenland](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/All/year/2024/tradeflow/Exports/partner/GRL/product/070320), [Arctic Import official](https://arcticimport.uk/), [KNI Engros official](https://kni.gl/en/kni-engros/), [Pilersuisoq official](https://kni.gl/en/pilersuisoq/), [KNI contact](https://kni.gl/en/contact/), [KK Engros official](https://kkengros.gl/hvem-er-kkengros/), [Brugseni official](https://brugseni.gl/), [Brugseni 2026 business profile](https://sulisitsisut.gl/en/nyheder/monthly-company-kalaallit-nunaanni-brugseni-brugseni-gl/), [Pisiffik official](https://www.pisiffik.gl/da/), [AKIKI official](https://akiki.gl/akiki/), [SPAR Greenland official](https://www.pisiffik.gl/da/content/32-spar), [Neqi official](https://kni.gl/en/neqi/), [Royal Greenland foodservice official](https://www.royalgreenland.com/en-us/foodservice/products/130867021/), [Polar Seafood official](https://www.polarseafood.com/contact), [Nuuk Transport Igaffik official](https://transport.gl/en/109-cafeteria-catering), [Mittarfeqarfiit aircraft/passenger handling](https://qr.mit.gl/en/services-en/aircraft-and-passenger-handling/), [Air Greenland food and beverages](https://kundeservice.airgreenland.gl/hc/en-us/articles/4950861012508-Food-and-beverages), [Hotel Arctic gastronomy](https://hotelarctic.com/en/gastronomy/), [Hotel Hans Egede official](https://hhe.gl/en/), [Albatros Arctic Circle official](https://albatros-arctic-circle.com/contact-us/), [World of Greenland / Ilimanaq Lodge restaurant](https://worldofgreenland.com/en/ilimanaq-lodge/restaurant/), [Hotel SOMA Restaurant SOMA Nuuk](https://hotelsoma.com/restaurant-nuuk/), [Nuuk Food public profile](https://www.foodbevg.com/GL/Nuuk/105731067833406/Nuuk-Food-Catering)
Signal quality: Medium-high for official Greenland grocery-wholesale, supermarket, institutional catering, frozen-food and tourism-foodservice channel evidence; low-to-medium for garlic-specific buyer ownership because public source data is mirror-export based and no Greenland-specific shipment buyer names were verified.
Interpretation note: WITS mirror-export data reports 2024 exports of fresh/chilled garlic to Greenland at USD 140.22K and 25,083 kg, with the European Union and Denmark as the visible source flow and a small Iceland flow. This is much lower than the legacy pipeline value, so reconcile trade values before market sizing. Official-source evidence is strongest for Arctic Import, KNI Engros, Pilersuisoq, KK Engros, Brugseni, Pisiffik, AKIKI and SPAR as route-to-market partners. Royal Greenland, Polar Seafood and Neqi support processed/frozen/ingredient use cases; Nuuk Transport, Mittarfeqarfiit, Air Greenland, Hotel Arctic, Hotel Hans Egede, Albatros Arctic Circle, World of Greenland, Hotel SOMA and Nuuk Food validate foodservice demand rather than direct importer ownership.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Arctic Import / KNI Engros / Pilersuisoq / KK Engros | Strongest Greenland route-to-market cluster. Arctic Import has the best contactable private B2B wholesale signal with 400000 product access, Nuuk warehousing, North Atlantic supply-chain expertise and catering supply. KNI Engros and Pilersuisoq add national public-service grocery/wholesale infrastructure. KK Engros adds professional-kitchen, hotel and restaurant foodservice supply. |
| 2 | Brugseni / Pisiffik / SPAR / AKIKI | Supermarket and grocery procurement cluster. Brugseni has stores across eight towns and Coop Denmark partnership evidence; Pisiffik has more than 40 stores in the six largest cities; SPAR validates fresh-grocery neighborhood stores; AKIKI validates discount grocery demand with 2500+ goods. Confirm centralized importer ownership before FCL quoting. |
| 3 | Royal Greenland / Polar Seafood / Neqi | Frozen, processed and ingredient cluster. Royal Greenland and Polar Seafood validate large-scale frozen-food, processor, retail-customer and foodservice ecosystems; Neqi validates local meat/crop processing and ready meals. Best fit is peeled refrigerated, frozen/IQF, prepared-food or ingredient garlic validation. |
| 4 | Nuuk Transport-Igaffik / Mittarfeqarfiit-Greenland Airports / Air Greenland | Institutional foodservice cluster. Public sources show one of Nuuk's largest cafeterias with company catering, airport catering via Hotel Kangerlussuaq and passenger meal service on Air Greenland routes. Use as demand validation or referral path through distributors. |
| 5 | Hotel Arctic / Hotel Hans Egede / Albatros Arctic Circle / World of Greenland / Hotel SOMA / Nuuk Food | Tourism, hotel and restaurant demand cluster. Official sources show premium hotel restaurants, banquets, conference catering, year-round lodges/restaurants and local-ingredient dining. Hotel SOMA has direct published garlic menu evidence. These are not first-pass FCL importers. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | No public Greenland-specific garlic shipment buyer names were verified. Paid data should identify beneficial importers behind Denmark/EU garlic flows and confirm whether Arctic Import, KNI, Pisiffik/Brugseni, KK Engros or another distributor owns the shipments. |

Signal quality: High for public mirror-export fresh-garlic demand and strong for official/public FMCG, fresh-produce, supermarket, frozen-food and HoReCa channel evidence; medium for direct beneficial buyer ownership because public Volza names need paid customs validation and several directory-verified contacts require confirmation before outreach.
Interpretation note: WITS mirror-export data reports 2023 exports of fresh/chilled garlic to DRC at USD 9.87M and 9.48M kg, led overwhelmingly by China. This is much higher than the legacy pipeline value, so reconcile trade values before sizing. Volza public pages report 110 DRC fresh-garlic buyers and 1185 fresh-garlic shipments, plus 382 broader garlic buyers and 8554 broader garlic shipments, with visible names including AFRICOMERCE, SUPERMARCHE SONIA SPRL, SOCIMEX SARL, ETS MUNIQUE, RAWJI and ETS JESSICA. Those names were not added as direct outreach leads unless official/public contact evidence was verified.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Elite Trading / Socimex / Beltexco / Kenshin / Hyper Psaro | Strongest DRC FCL-style import-distribution and fresh/frozen route-to-market cluster. Elite provides the clearest official fresh-produce and imported-vegetable cold-chain evidence; Socimex adds national basic food and frozen-food importing; Beltexco adds the largest FMCG distribution signal; Kenshin adds importer/distributor/supermarket operation; Hyper Psaro adds fresh/frozen storage, distribution and Carrefour retail scale. |
| 2 | Kin Marche / City Market / Monishop / SN MART / Carrefour Hyper Psaro | Supermarket and retail procurement cluster. Official/public sources show 60+ stores, wholesale/semi-wholesale, agro-food wholesale/retail, modern supermarket fresh-food categories and Carrefour franchise expansion. Validate direct importer ownership before FCL quoting. |
| 3 | AFRIFOOD / Congo Futur / Afriglobe / Hasson Africa / Alimentation Sieco | FMCG import, wholesale and branch-network cluster. Public and official sources show 55+ stores, 7+ warehouses, food importation/wholesale, nationwide food/non-food distribution, hypermarkets, trucks, multiple channels and food import/export directory evidence. |
| 4 | SO GOOD RDC / IMEXCO / SOLE Food / Etablissements Sangano | Frozen, HoReCa and institutional foodservice cluster. Sources show fresh/frozen categories, frozen fries, seafood, poultry, canned vegetables, sauces, pasta, Italian food imports for professionals, HoReCa/retail supply and institutional food supply to mines, NGOs, hotels and hospitals. Best fit is peeled refrigerated, frozen/IQF or foodservice garlic validation. |
| 5 | Sol Agro Alimentaire / Asali Foods / Etablissements Sangano | Processed and ingredient cluster. Sol Agro validates national branded-food distribution, Asali validates sauces and spice bouillon, and Sangano validates spices plus institutional food supply. Use for processed, paste, minced, dehydrated or seasoning garlic prospects. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Volza exposes a large DRC garlic-buyer universe, including fresh-garlic buyer names, but public contact validation is incomplete. Paid data should confirm beneficial importers behind China-led flows and decide whether Socimex, Rawji/Beltexco, Hyper Psaro, Elite, AFRICOMERCE, SUPERMARCHE SONIA SPRL, ETS MUNIQUE, ETS JESSICA or another operator owns the shipments. |

Signal quality: High for official fresh/chilled garlic import demand and unusually strong for direct public garlic product evidence; medium for beneficial importer ownership because many public leads are route-to-market channels and public shipment pages need paid customs validation before outreach.
Interpretation note: WITS reports Fiji 2023 imports of fresh/chilled garlic at USD 4.79M and 2.61M kg, led by China, Singapore, Hong Kong, China, the United States and India. Direct public garlic evidence exists at Fresh'et for frozen peeled garlic, FarmBoy for fresh garlic imp, MH for a retail garlic SKU and public Volza HSN 0712 pages for RANS garlic-powder imports. The key commercial question is not demand, but ownership of the main fresh-garlic import flows and whether those flows are controlled by produce importers, FMCG distributors or supermarket groups.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Fresh'et / FarmBoy / Turners & Growers Fiji / Fruit Core / Jack's Farms | Fresh'et has direct frozen peeled garlic evidence and broad cold-chain redistribution to hotels, supermarkets, hospitals and government sectors. FarmBoy has direct Fresh Garlic IMP evidence. Turners & Growers Fiji is the strongest scaled fresh whole garlic candidate because public profiles describe it as Fiji's leading fresh fruit and vegetable importer with weekly chilled/frozen imports and supermarket, foodservice and resort customers. Fruit Core and Jack's Farms add produce logistics and foodservice/retail supply. |
| 2 | Yee's / Five Star Foods-Markwell / Ontime Catering | Foodservice, resort, airline, yacht and institutional distribution cluster. Yee's validates freezer trucks, ambient/cool warehousing and major-supermarket distribution; Five Star/Markwell validates direct imports, chiller trucks and near-market-wide resort/restaurant/superyacht coverage; Ontime validates Fijiwide distribution to wholesalers, hotels, airlines, supermarkets, bakeries and food manufacturers. |
| 3 | CJ Patel / Punjas / NewWorld IGA / RB Patel / MH / Extra / Cost-U-Less / Rajendras | FMCG, supermarket and warehouse-retail cluster. CJ Patel and Punjas are the strongest broadline distribution platforms; NewWorld, RB Patel, MH, Extra, Cost-U-Less and Rajendras validate supermarket procurement, wholesale arms, warehouse retail and national consumer demand. MH adds direct retail garlic SKU evidence. |
| 4 | RANS Import Export / Shop N Save / Rajendra Prasad Fiji shipment signals | Public shipment-signal cluster. Volza exposes RANS as a garlic-powder importer under HSN 0712, and public trade pages identify Rajendra Prasad Fiji Pte Limited as a large importer. Validate RANS/Shop N Save ownership linkage and fresh-garlic beneficial importer identity before treating either as a direct fresh whole FCL buyer. |
| 5 | Goodman Fielder / Foods Pacific / Food Processors Fiji | Processor and ingredient cluster. Goodman Fielder validates industrial, retail-chain and foodservice reach; Foods Pacific validates sauces, spices and foodservice products; Food Processors Fiji validates frozen vegetables, sauces, canned foods and agro-processing. Best fit is processed, minced, paste, dehydrated, frozen/IQF or ingredient garlic validation. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should identify beneficial importers behind China/Singapore/Hong Kong-led fresh-garlic flows and determine whether T&G, Fresh'et, CJ Patel, Punjas, NewWorld, RB Patel, MH, Extra, Rajendras, RANS/Shop N Save or another buyer owns the containerized garlic shipments. |

Signal quality: Medium-high for public import demand and Benin route-to-market mapping; medium for direct beneficial buyer ownership because public garlic-specific company evidence is mostly trade-directory based and several strong official-source leads are broad food distributors or retail procurement channels.
Interpretation note: WITS reports Benin 2023 imports of fresh/chilled garlic at USD 107.70K and 469,970 kg, led by China, Egypt, France, Togo, Niger and Nigeria. This is materially lower than the legacy pipeline value, so reconcile trade values before market sizing. | Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Kaba Group / CBND / IDESCO / ACOR / BTG / Continental Inter Commercial | Strongest Benin import-distribution cluster. | 2 | Erevan / Mayfair / UNIDIS / Attidza / L'Etoile / Star Lights / Yamaya / Benin Market | Supermarket and retail procurement cluster. Public distribution references identify several as active Benin supermarket chains, and public directories provide contacts. Erevan/Super U has official modern-retail platform evidence. | 3 | Soulmate Corporation / Jajolinks / EMCO Benin | Direct public garlic, spices and food-import signal cluster. Soulmate explicitly lists fresh garlic among imported foodline products; Jajolinks lists spices and garlic; EMCO lists food, beverages and spices as importer/distributor product categories. These are product-fit leads but need current-activity and customs validation. |
| 4 | Fludor Benin / Bellemira Agroalimentaire / Aliments Benin | Processor, ingredient and local food-platform cluster. Fludor validates large agro-industrial food processing, Bellemira validates spices, sauces, purees, natural bouillons, wholesale and distribution representation, and Aliments Benin validates online fresh-food/spices procurement. Best fit is processed, paste, minced, dehydrated or seasoning garlic validation. |
| 5 | External export-to-Benin route intelligence | Miamland and CABF public pages show structured export/wholesale programs serving Benin supermarket, hospitality, restaurant and retail channels with fresh, frozen and dry products. Use these as channel intelligence only, not as Benin-based direct buyer records. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should identify beneficial importers behind China/Egypt/France/Togo/Niger/Nigeria fresh-garlic flows and determine whether Kaba, CBND, Erevan, UNIDIS, Soulmate, Jajolinks, EMCO or another buyer owns the containerized shipments. |

| 2 | Diamond Food / Cheaitou Brothers / National Frozen Food / T R H Trading / Africa 1 / West Africa / Banjak / Caroline / Himmie / Carl Trading | Frozen and cold-chain cluster. Diamond publishes frozen food and frozen vegetable product evidence. Cheaitou publishes official frozen-food, fish, dry-goods, branch and purchase/sales contacts. National Frozen, TRH, Africa 1, West Africa, Banjak and Caroline add public shipment-signal evidence; Himmie and Carl add frozen-food directory coverage. Best fit is peeled refrigerated or frozen/IQF validation unless paid data confirms fresh-garlic ownership. |
| 3 | AgroMArket / U.N. Drive Supermarket / Greenland Suppermarket / Shop Rite General Merchandise / Stop & Shop / Exclusive Supermarket | Direct garlic and retail demand cluster. AgroMArket has the clearest public direct garlic evidence through its official vegetable and fruit marketplace. U.N. Drive, Greenland, Shop Rite, Stop & Shop and Exclusive validate supermarket or grocery demand and potential distributor referrals, but are lower priority than import/distribution platforms for FCL outreach. |
| 4 | Public shipment-signal target discovery | Public shipment pages expose National Frozen Food Corporation, T R H Trading Corporation, Africa 1 Enterprise, West Africa Enterprise, Banjak Frozen Food and Caroline Frozen Food as Liberia importers or frozen-food buyers. Use paid data to test HS 070320 and supplier overlap before outreach. |
| 5 | Paid customs-data reconciliation | Paid data should reconcile WITS direct imports against mirror-export values and identify whether Harbel, A-Z, AJA/Fresh Frozen, Diamond, Cheaitou, National Frozen, TRH or another Monrovia operator owns the containerized fresh-garlic lane. |

## Fresh Garlic Import, Supermarket, Distribution And Foodservice Buyer Signal - El Salvador

Source: [WITS - El Salvador fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/SLV/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/ALL/product/070320), [Super Selectos official](https://www.superselectos.com/nosotros), [PriceSmart El Salvador official](https://www.pricesmart.com/es-SV), [Walmart El Salvador fresh produce](https://www.walmart.com.sv/frutas-y-verduras), [DISZASA official services](https://www.diszasa.com/en/services/), [DISNA official distribution channels](https://disna.com.sv/en/distribution-channels/), [UDISA official distribution channels](https://www.udisa.com.sv/en/distribution-channels/), [Multicongelados official](https://multicongelados.net/en/), [DIACO Foods official channels](https://diacofoods.sv/canales-de-venta/), [DISALCO official](https://disalco.sv/), [Distribuidora Morazán official](https://www.distribuidoramorazan.com/aboutus), [DeSantis official](https://desantisdistribuidora.com/about-us), [Distribuidores Clave official](https://distribuidoresclave.com/), [ARES restaurant supplier directory](https://ares.sv/socios-proveedores/), [Legumé Foods official](https://www.legumefoods.com/quienes-somos/), [PIMI official](https://www.pimi.com.sv/), [SUMIN official](https://suminsv.com/), [Ecofoods government exporter directory](https://investinelsalvador.gob.sv/directorio/ecofoods-s-a-de-c-v/), [McCormick government exporter directory](https://investinelsalvador.gob.sv/directorio/mccormick-de-centro-america-s-a-de-c-v/), [SISAM garlic-product registry example](https://sisam.srs.gob.sv/admin/centeot/registrosanitario/alimproductolist/64511/showProducto), [GuGadir spice distributor directory](https://elsalvador.gugadir.com/distribuidores-de-especias-el-salvador/)
Signal quality: High for public garlic import demand and retail SKU evidence; medium for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because the strongest public names are supermarket, warehouse-club, distribution and foodservice channels and only paid customs can identify the main HS 070320 buyer behind China-led flows.
Interpretation note: WITS reports El Salvador 2023 imports of fresh/chilled garlic at USD 2.75M and 2.53M kg, led by China at USD 2.59M and 2.33M kg, with smaller Guatemala and Peru flows. Public evidence is unusually strong: Super Selectos and Walmart expose direct fresh-garlic retail SKUs, PriceSmart has public peeled/minced/garlic-product evidence and SISAM registrations, and DISZASA, DISNA, UDISA, Multicongelados and DIACO show national distributor or cold-chain routes that could handle container-volume sales.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Super Selectos / PriceSmart / Walmart El Salvador | Strongest direct garlic and retail procurement cluster. Super Selectos combines more than 110 stores, all-department coverage, foreign-supplier buying relationships and direct fresh-garlic evidence. PriceSmart adds peeled-garlic and garlic-product registration/import evidence. Walmart adds fresh garlic and Hortifruti-linked produce procurement infrastructure. Validate which entity owns the bulk HS 070320 lane before quoting FCL volume. |
| 2 | DISZASA / DISNA / UDISA / Distribuidora Morazán / DISALCO | National distributor cluster. DISZASA has cold-chain warehousing, trucks/containers and nationwide multi-channel coverage; DISNA and UDISA expose broad supermarket, wholesale, route and HORECA coverage; Morazán and DISALCO add mass-consumption, culinary-brand and condiment distribution. Best use is FCL route-to-market validation and distributor referrals. |
| 3 | Multicongelados / DIACO Foods / DeSantis / Distribuidores Clave / Comercializadora de Alimentos | Frozen, IQF and foodservice cluster. Multicongelados and DIACO show official frozen-products and foodservice coverage, DeSantis and Distribuidores Clave show frozen import/distribution and supermarket or foodservice cold-chain coverage, and Comercializadora de Alimentos is exposed through the ARES supplier directory. Best fit is peeled refrigerated and frozen/IQF garlic validation. |
| 4 | Legumé Foods / PIMI / SUMIN / Ecofoods / McCormick de Centro América | Fresh-vegetable, foodservice ingredient and processor cluster. Legumé validates retail/foodservice vegetables and value-added fresh foods; PIMI and SUMIN validate foodservice and specialty ingredient demand; Ecofoods and McCormick validate processed-food, spices, sauce and garlic-seasoning channels. Best fit is processed, minced, peeled or ingredient garlic rather than fresh whole FCL. |
| 5 | Grupo Alimenticio / Importcolor / SISAM registry discovery | Direct public garlic and spice signals. Public directories identify Grupo Alimenticio as a spice and condiment importer/distributor including garlic and Importcolor as an ingredient importer/distributor handling garlic and onion powders. SISAM registrations expose garlic-product import/distribution signals. These are validation targets only until current activity and customs data are confirmed. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should identify beneficial importers behind China-led fresh-garlic shipments and determine whether Super Selectos/Calleja, PriceSmart, Walmart/Hortifruti, DISZASA, DISNA, UDISA, Morazán, Multicongelados, DIACO or another Salvadoran operator owns the containerized garlic flow. |

## Fresh Garlic Import, Food Distribution And Supermarket Buyer Signal - Burkina Faso

Source: [WITS - Burkina Faso fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/BFA/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/ALL/product/070320), [SODIMA official](https://sodimabf.com/), [SIDAF official](https://sidafcisa.com/), [GECOM-BURKINA official](https://gks-international-bf.com/filiales/gecom-burkina), [SODIAL official distribution page](https://sodial.sn/en/distribution-alimentaire/), [Les Primeurs du Faso official](https://lesprimeursdufaso.bf/), [HYANTA official](https://hyanta.com/), [BurkinaMarket fresh-garlic listing](https://burkinamarket.net/product/ail-1-kg-frais/), [Marina Market public profile](https://www.africabizinfo.com/fr-BF/marina-market-burkina-25-31-28-80), [SDA public profile](https://www.goafricaonline.com/bf/19620-sda-grossiste-en-alimentation-ouagadougou-burkina-faso), [SANYA Distribution public profile](https://www.goafricaonline.com/bf/1197681-sanya-distribution-alimentation-generale-ouagadougou-burkina-faso), [SOKA Distribution public profile](https://www.businesses10.com/BF/Ouagadougou/233439018623620/SOKA-Distribution_BF-SARL), [SANO GLOBAL official](https://sanoglobal.info/), [Dan Group official](https://groupdan.com/), [Go Africa Online Burkina Faso food directory](https://www.goafricaonline.com/bf/annuaire/alimentation-generale)
Signal quality: Medium for public import demand and route-to-market mapping; medium-low for confirmed beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because most public leads expose food-import, wholesale, fresh-produce, supermarket or frozen-food channels rather than named HS 070320 shipment ownership.
Interpretation note: WITS reports Burkina Faso 2023 imports of fresh/chilled garlic at USD 476.72K and 4.06M kg, led by China at USD 456.32K and 3.88M kg, with smaller South Africa, India and Cote d'Ivoire flows. Public sources now map scaled food importers and distributors, direct fresh-garlic marketplace evidence and supermarket/fresh-produce demand, but paid customs data is required before quoting FCL volume or treating any company as the beneficial importer behind China-led garlic flows.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | SODIMA / SIDAF / GECOM-BURKINA / SODIAL | Strongest FCL-style import and distribution validation cluster. SODIMA has official food importation, large warehousing and sub-regional distribution evidence. SIDAF has official food-commodity import/distribution and Burkina office evidence. GECOM-BURKINA has direct international sourcing, 25,000 tons of storage and multiple warehouses. SODIAL validates regional food supply, logistics, cold rooms and an Ouagadougou operational structure. |
| 2 | Les Primeurs du Faso / HYANTA / BurkinaMarket / Marina Market | Fresh-produce, direct garlic and supermarket demand cluster. Les Primeurs and HYANTA validate fresh-produce procurement and delivery channels. BurkinaMarket provides direct Ail 1 kg frais evidence. Marina Market adds supermarket and wholesale-style demand across Ouagadougou and Bobo-Dioulasso. Use this cluster for demand checks and distributor referrals. |
| 3 | SDA / SANYA / SOKA / SANO GLOBAL / Dan Group-Graine d'Or | Wholesale, frozen-food, import-export and sourcing cluster. SDA is a public food-wholesaler listing, SANYA sells dry and frozen foods, SOKA is a purchasing center/import-export agrofood operator, SANO GLOBAL validates Asian food-product sourcing and Burkina clearance, and Dan Group's Graine d'Or validates agrofood import-export of essential foods. |
| 4 | SCIMAS / SBFA / IECG / SHOPETTE / ESAF / Global Multi Service Negoce / CGC | Directory-only general-food route cluster. These are lower-certainty public food-channel records. Global Multi Service Negoce is the best import-specific record because the listing explicitly states general-food and beverage importation. Validate current activity before outreach. |
| 5 | Public import signal and reconciliation | WITS shows high physical volume but lower value than the legacy pipeline, so market sizing should be reconciled before outreach. Prioritize SODIMA, SIDAF and GECOM-BURKINA for FCL validation because their official evidence is stronger than supermarket or directory-only leads. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should identify beneficial importers behind China-led HS 070320 flows and test whether SODIMA, SIDAF, GECOM-BURKINA, Marina Market, SDA, SANYA, SOKA or another Ouagadougou/Bobo-Dioulasso operator owns containerized fresh-garlic shipments. |

## Fresh Garlic Import, Shipment-Signal, Food Distribution And Supermarket Buyer Signal - Tanzania

Source: [WITS - Tanzania fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/TZA/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/ALL/product/070320), [Volza Tanzania fresh-garlic buyers](https://www.volza.com/p/fresh-garlic/buyers/buyers-in-tanzania/), [Sunvallei Volza company profile](https://www.volza.com/company-profile/sunvallei-international-limited-73227437/), [LICO Global official](https://lico.co.tz/index.php/266-2/), [Mountain Taste official](https://mountaintaste.co.tz/about/), [East Africa Fruits / Soko Chap official](https://www.soko-chap.com/contact), [Kilimanjaro Flavours official](https://kilimanjaroflavours.co.tz/contact-us/), [Buildmart official](https://buildmart.co.tz/), [Mega Mart / Premium Supplies official](https://www.megamart.co.tz/), [Shoppers official](https://www.shoppers.co.tz/), [ChapMart imported-garlic listing](https://chapmart.com/products/preview/1887/imported-garlic-per-kg?currency=TZS), [Village Supermarket official](https://villagesupermarket.co.tz/about/), [Suvacor official](https://www.suvacor.com/about/), [Afrione official](https://afrioneholdings.co.tz/), [Prime Route official](https://www.primeroute.co.tz/), [Canary Industrials official](https://www.canaryindustrials.com/), [Med Foods official](https://medfoods.co.tz/), [Tanganyika Food Resources official](https://tanganyikafoods.com/), [Heaven Distributor official](https://heavendistributor.co.tz/), [Garden Market official](https://gardenmarket.co.tz/), [ShambaFresh official](https://shambafresh.co.tz/)
Signal quality: Medium-high for public garlic demand because WITS confirms direct fresh/chilled garlic imports and Volza exposes Tanzania fresh-garlic buyer names; medium for contactability because the strongest shipment-signal buyers do not expose official contact details in public sources.
Interpretation note: WITS reports Tanzania 2023 imports of fresh/chilled garlic at USD 458.98K and 733,960 kg, led by China at USD 458.02K and 728,108 kg, with smaller United Arab Emirates, Kenya and Japan flows. Volza public buyer summaries identify Sunvallei International and Zamjoy Investments as fresh-garlic buyer signals, and official company sources now map contactable import-export, fresh-produce, HORECA, supermarket, FMCG and ingredient routes. Paid customs data is required before quoting full-container volume or treating any public source as beneficial importer ownership.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Sunvallei International / Zamjoy Investments / LICO Global / Mountain Taste | Strongest mixed shipment and contactable validation cluster. Volza exposes Sunvallei and Zamjoy as Tanzania fresh-garlic buyer signals, with Sunvallei sample records for fresh white garlic from China. LICO is the strongest contact-ready official import-export lead for spices and fresh products. Mountain Taste adds direct garlic processing and trading evidence. |
| 2 | East Africa Fruits / FFTT Zanzibar / Garden Market / ShambaFresh | Fresh-produce and foodservice demand cluster. East Africa Fruits/Soko Chap supplies produce to restaurants, vendors, stores, businesses and homes. FFTT supplies fresh produce to Zanzibar restaurants and hotels. Garden Market and ShambaFresh add online grocery and fresh-produce procurement signals. |
| 3 | Shoppers / Village Supermarket / Buildmart / Premium Supplies / Suvacor | Supermarket, HORECA and broadline distribution cluster. Shoppers has direct public imported-garlic retail evidence. Village Supermarket validates fresh produce and international grocery demand. Buildmart, Premium Supplies and Suvacor validate imported food, ingredients, condiments, spices, wholesale, hotels, restaurants, lodges, supermarkets and institutional channels. |
| 4 | Afrione / Prime Route / Canary / Med Foods / Heaven Distributor | FMCG, foodservice and wholesale route cluster. Afrione, Prime Route and Canary validate food-and-beverage or FMCG distribution, sourcing, logistics and B2B supply. Med Foods adds Arusha import, wholesale, retail and HORECA distribution. Heaven Distributor adds general food-product and beverage distribution. |
| 5 | Kilimanjaro Flavours / Tanganyika Food Resources | Processed garlic, spices and ingredient cluster. Kilimanjaro Flavours publishes direct garlic and garlic-powder evidence. Tanganyika Food Resources validates local agro-products, spices and herbs processing and packaging. Best fit is garlic powder, dehydrated, seasoning, processed or ingredient garlic. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should reconcile WITS and Volza, identify the beneficial importers behind China-led HS 070320 flows, and test whether Sunvallei, Zamjoy, LICO, Shoppers, Buildmart, Suvacor, East Africa Fruits or another operator owns containerized fresh-garlic shipments. |

Signal quality: Medium for Monaco route-to-market mapping; medium-low for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because public sources mostly identify provisioners, supermarkets, hospitality buyers, brokers and directory-listed food wholesalers rather than shipment-level garlic importers.

Validate procurement ownership because these routes may be controlled through French/regional parent networks. |
| 4 | Monte-Carlo SBM / Fairmont Monte Carlo / Le Méridien Beach Plaza | High-value hotel, event and restaurant foodservice cluster. These groups validate premium foodservice demand through restaurants, catering, banquets, events, meetings and room service. Best use is distributor referral and peeled/foodservice garlic validation. |
| 5 | AGAP International / Ital Passion / Mercure International / M.D.V. / La Monégasque | Broker, import-export, ship-supply and cold-chain cluster. Useful for frozen/IQF, processed, condiment or mixed-food route validation, but lower certainty for fresh whole garlic ownership. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs and French/Italian/EU routing data should determine whether garlic shipments are booked under Monaco companies, French supermarket parent procurement, regional provisioners or yacht-supply operators. Test Monafrais, Les Primeurs, Monaco Trade, Monaco Foods, Carrefour, Just Provisions and Catalano before quoting FCL volume. |

Source: [WITS Burundi fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/BDI/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/WLD/product/070320), [WITS Burundi imports from Tanzania](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/BDI/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/TZA/product/070320), [Volza dry/garlic Burundi buyer summary](https://www.volza.com/p/dry-or-garlic/buyers/buyers-in-burundi/), [MARIMBU official](https://www.marimbu.com/), [Kilakitu official](https://kilakitu.bi/cms/view/1), [Burundi Shop official](https://burundishop.bi/), [Burundi Fortified Foods official](https://www.burundifortified.com/contact/), [Taste Burundi official](https://tasteburundi.com/), [Fruito Burundi official](https://fruitoburundi.com/contact.php), [Baza Burundi official](https://baza.bi/), [Rehema Shop official](https://rehema-shop.onrender.com/), [Cybo Bujumbura wholesale food directory](https://www.cybo.com/BI/bujumbura/wholesale-of-food%2C-beverages-and-tobacco/), [Cybo Bujumbura food directory](https://www.cybo.com/BI/bujumbura/food/), [Cybo Bujumbura food directory page 2](https://www.cybo.com/BI/bujumbura/food/?p=2), [Cybo grocery directory](https://www.cybo.com/BI/rambwe/grocery-stores-and-supermarkets/), [East Africa Manufacturing directory PDF](https://eamanufact.com/publications/Eamanufact2022.pdf)
Signal quality: Medium for Burundi route-to-market mapping; medium-low for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because public sources identify import redistribution, e-commerce grocery platforms, processors, supermarkets and local grocery demand more clearly than shipment-level garlic owners.

This materially undercuts the legacy pipeline value of USD 1.36M and points to a regional Tanzania/Rwanda/Bujumbura cross-border lane rather than a large directly visible deep-sea garlic importer. Paid data is required before quoting full-container volume.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | MARIMBU / Elite Enterprises / Sam grocery store | Strongest import/wholesale route cluster. MARIMBU is the best contactable lead because official sources show food-product importation, redistribution and warehouse-backed distribution. | 2 | Kilakitu / Burundi Shop-Haraka / Baza Burundi / Rehema Shop | Grocery platform and demand-aggregation cluster. Kilakitu and Burundi Shop provide organized e-commerce, seller-network, delivery and trade/logistics routes. Baza adds restaurant delivery plus grocery, spices and food-cupboard categories. Rehema adds direct online supermarket vegetable and grocery demand. |
| 3 | Au Bon Prix / Google Supermarket / Sun city store-Pride of Burundi / Home World / Casino / Kigobe | Supermarket demand cluster. These public supermarket and grocery records validate imported packaged-food and fresh-grocery demand in Bujumbura and Gitega, but procurement ownership is unclear and likely smaller than MARIMBU-style import/redistribution routes. |
| 4 | Burundi Fortified Foods / Fruito Burundi / Taste Burundi | Processor and produce-channel cluster. Burundi Fortified Foods validates food-manufacturing procurement, Fruito validates agro-food processing and wholesale/retail distribution, and Taste Burundi validates fresh produce, vegetables and herbs through farm and partner-farmer networks. |
| 5 | Fatima Grocery / Boucherie Nouvelle / BMC Grocery / Hobe Snack Bar Minimarket | Local grocery, minimarket and foodservice cluster. Useful for demand checks, distributor referrals and small-format grocery validation. These leads are lower priority for full-container sales unless paid customs data or local distributor referral shows import ownership. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs should identify whether Burundi garlic flows are booked under MARIMBU, regional Tanzania/Rwanda traders, Bujumbura wholesalers, supermarkets, grocery platforms or individual Volza-listed importers. Test HS 070320, 071080, 071190, 071290 and dry-garlic keywords before quoting FCL volume. |

Interpretation note: Hong Kong should be handled as both a consumption market and possible re-export/transshipment node. The strongest outreach-first leads are contactable fresh-produce and foodservice distributors, but full-container sizing should wait until customs data separates direct importers from downstream distributors, supermarkets and marketplace sellers.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | The Food Source / Wing Kee Produce / Chef's Garden / Fresh Channels / C.Y. Food Trading | Fresh-produce importer and wholesale cluster. The Food Source combines direct import, exclusive distribution, global sourcing and institutional channels. Wing Kee adds 50-country produce import, coldroom/warehouse and air/sea freight evidence. Chef's Garden, Fresh Channels and C.Y. Food Trading add wholesale-market, hotel, catering, airline, restaurant and chain-restaurant produce coverage. |
| 2 | DCH Foods / Sims Trading / Angliss / Classic Fine Foods / Foodgears / Lamex / Team River | Broadline, foodservice, frozen and grocery distribution cluster. DCH and Sims are strongest for scaled agency/FMCG and Hong Kong-Macao route coverage. Angliss and Classic validate premium foodservice demand. Foodgears and Lamex support grocery, seasoning, frozen and vegetable-trading routes. Team River adds fresh/frozen sourcing and Western Wholesale Food Market access. |
| 3 | Lee Kum Kee / FoodCraft / Win Fresh Life Style / PURE FRESH / Unionsum | Direct garlic and processed/peeled garlic cluster. Lee Kum Kee gives foodservice minced/dehydrated garlic evidence. FoodCraft lists fresh peeled garlic, black garlic and fermented garlic paste. Win Fresh lists peeled garlic and wholesale fresh produce to restaurants and chain stores. PURE FRESH and Unionsum add fresh-garlic and black-garlic marketplace demand. |
| 4 | Wellcome / city'super / Kai Bo Food Supermarket | Supermarket procurement and demand cluster. Wellcome publishes fresh garlic and chilled peeled garlic SKUs. city'super validates premium garlic/onion/leek and gourmet grocery demand. Kai Bo validates large-format Hong Kong food-supermarket demand with fresh vegetables, fruit, frozen poultry, meat, seafood and grocery categories. |
| 5 | Public shipment and trade-data reconciliation | WITS direct imports, China mirror exports and Volza public snippets point to inconsistent public sizing. Treat public trade figures as directional until paid data identifies which entities own Hong Kong garlic entries and whether shipments are local consumption, re-export or mixed foodservice/retail consolidation. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs should test HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF, dehydrated and minced garlic keywords against The Food Source, Wing Kee, DCH, Sims, Angliss, Classic, Foodgears, Lamex, Fresh Channels, C.Y. Food Trading, Wellcome, Kai Bo, citysuper, Win Fresh, PURE FRESH and Lee Kum Kee before quoting FCL volume. |

## Fresh Garlic Import, Wholesale, Supermarket And Hospitality Buyer Signal - Samoa

Source: [WITS Samoa fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/ALL/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/WLD/product/070320), [WITS Samoa imports from China](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/ALL/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/CHN/product/070320), [Ah Liki official](https://www.ahliki.com/services), [Frankie official](https://frankiesamoa.com/pages/about-us), [SSAB official](https://ssab.ws/pages/homepage), [Gounders official](https://gounderssamoa.com/pages/gounders-samoa-wholesalers-distributor-in-samoa), [Chan Mow Wholesale public page](https://www.foodbevg.com/WS/Apia/1310072682451793/Chan-Mow-Wholesale), [Chan Mow Garlic 1kg via My Samoa](https://www.mysamoa.co/products/garlic-1kg), [Farmer Joe via Ah Liki](https://www.ahliki.com/services), [Lucky Foodtown Cybo](https://www.cybo.com/WS-biz/lucky-food-town), [Lynn's Supermarket public listing](https://wanderlog.com/place/details/2905002/lynns-supermarket--bakery), [Myna's Supermarket listing](https://www.talofakids.com/shopping/myna%27s-supermarket), [Mariyon Cybo](https://www.cybo.com/WS-biz/mariyon-samoa-supermarket-lotopa), [Maota supermarket Cybo](https://www.cybo.com/WS/siumu/grocery-stores-and-supermarkets/), [Fugalei Fresh Produce Market official tourism](https://www.samoa.travel/plan-book/activities/fugalei-fresh-produce-market/), [Maua App official](https://skyeyepacific.com/maua/download-the-maua-app/), [Sheraton Samoa dining/events](https://www.marriott.com/en-us/hotels/apwsi-sheraton-samoa-aggie-greys-hotel-and-bungalows/events/), [Taumeasina dining](https://www.taumeasinaislandresortsamoa.com/dining), [Lava Hotel dining](https://www.lavahotel.ws/dining), [Seabreeze restaurant](https://seabreezesamoa.com/restaurant/), [Sinalei restaurant tourism page](https://www.samoa.travel/plan-book/activities/ava-i-toga-restaurant-sinalei-reef-resort-spa/), [Tanoa Tusitala official contact](https://www.tanoahotels.com/samoa-tusitala-hotel/contact/)
Signal quality: Medium-high for Samoa route-to-market mapping because public official sources identify national food distribution, wholesale, supermarkets, direct garlic retail evidence, online grocery/delivery and large hospitality buyers; medium-low for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because public sources do not identify shipment owners.
Interpretation note: WITS reports Samoa 2023 imports of fresh/chilled garlic at USD 242.27K and 157,181 kg, with China supplying USD 171.36K and 85,642 kg. This is well below the legacy pipeline value of USD 1.30M, so Samoa should be approached as a conservative mixed-container or smaller FCL market until paid data confirms the import owner and true volume.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Ah Liki / Frankie / SSAB / Gounders / Chan Mow Wholesale / Farmer Joe | Core food-distribution and supermarket cluster. Ah Liki is strongest because official sources combine food distribution, globally sourced bulk foodstuff, supermarkets, farms, food manufacturing and full-market coverage. Frankie and SSAB add national supermarket and wholesale grocery scale. Gounders, Chan Mow Wholesale and Farmer Joe add bulk grocery, food-wholesale and supermarket routes. |
| 2 | Chan Mow Supermarket / Lucky Foodtown / Lynn's / Myna's / Mariyon / Maota / Fugalei Market | Retail and fresh-produce demand cluster. Chan Mow provides direct Garlic 1kg public product evidence. Lucky, Lynn's, Myna's, Mariyon and Maota validate supermarket demand, while Fugalei Market maps central fresh-produce demand and distributor-referral routes. |
| 3 | Maua App / My Samoa | E-commerce and vendor-discovery cluster. Maua identifies local produce, food, seafood and delivery categories; My Samoa exposes vendor-level grocery SKUs and delivery/pickup workflows. Useful for demand checks and local seller discovery, but not first-pass FCL importer ownership. |
| 4 | Sheraton Samoa Aggie Grey's / Taumeasina / Lava / Seabreeze / Sinalei / Tanoa | Hospitality and event foodservice cluster. These hotels and resorts validate restaurant, bar, buffet, banquet, wedding, private dining and room-service demand. Tanoa has direct menu garlic-use evidence, while Sheraton has the strongest event scale signal. |
| 5 | Public trade-data reconciliation | WITS direct-import data is materially below the legacy pipeline value. Treat Samoa demand as real but conservative until customs data verifies whether imports are concentrated under Ah Liki/Frankie/Chan Mow/Gounders or split across mixed supermarket containers and China/New Zealand supply routes. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs should test Ah Liki, Frankie, SSAB, Gounders, Chan Mow, Farmer Joe, supermarket chains and hotel-procurement channels across HS 070320, peeled/chilled garlic, frozen/IQF garlic and dehydrated/minced garlic before quoting FCL volume. |

Interpretation note: Curaçao has stronger public shipment-signal discovery than many Tier 3 islands because Volza/Panjiva/ImportGenius snippets expose named fruit, vegetable, lettuce and food-ingredient buyers. However, those signals must be reconciled with official distributor coverage before assigning FCL garlic ownership.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Hector Henriquez / Curaçao Foods Trade / EWT / Pietersz / DeliNova / Marquis | Core distribution and wholesale cluster. Hector Henriquez remains the strongest contactable lead because official sources show food, foodservice and institutional divisions. | 2 | Orzal Fruits / Fresh Prods and Logistics / Carrot Export Foods & Drinks / FUIK Microgreens | Fresh-produce importer and shipment-signal cluster. Public Volza, Panjiva and ImportGenius snippets expose HS 07/08, fruit, vegetable, tomato, melon, lettuce or fresh-produce shipment signals. These names should be first-pass paid-data targets for HS 070320. |
| 3 | Goisco / Van den Tweel / Centrum / Mangusa / Vreugdenhil / Carrefour / Bon Bini | Supermarket and warehouse-club demand cluster. Goisco has warehouse-club scale plus public grocery/vegetable import snippets. The rest validate fresh produce, frozen food, supermarket, wholesale grocery, premium import and mass-retail demand. |
| 4 | Barentz-Deltagen / Salty Seafood / Arcos Dorados-McDonald's | Frozen, ingredient and institutional foodservice cluster. Barentz/Deltagen supports restaurants, hotels, catering and supermarkets with baked/frozen products and ingredients. Salty Seafood supports restaurants, hotels, yachting and supermarkets. Arcos Dorados/McDonald's adds institutional QSR scale and public lettuce-fresh buyer signals. |
| 5 | Public shipment-signal reconciliation | Volza and Panjiva snippets identify Fresh Prods, Carrot, Orzal, FUIK, DeliNova, Goisco and Arcos/Golden Arches in adjacent produce, vegetable, food-ingredient or lettuce flows. Treat this as target-discovery intelligence, not direct garlic proof. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs should test Hector Henriquez, Curaçao Foods Trade, EWT, Pietersz, DeliNova, Goisco, Orzal, Fresh Prods, Carrot, FUIK, Centrum, Mangusa and supermarket procurement channels across HS 070320, peeled/chilled garlic, frozen/IQF garlic and dehydrated/minced garlic before quoting FCL volume. |

Source: [WITS Djibouti fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/DJI/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/ALL/product/070320), [Volza Djibouti garlic buyers](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic/buyers/buyers-in-djibouti/), [Volza ginger/garlic buyers](https://www.volza.com/p/ginger-or-garlic/buyers/buyers-in-djibouti/), [BMMI official divisions](https://www.bmmigroup.com/divisions/), [BMMI Djibouti office directory](https://www.bmmigroup.com/contact-us/office-directory/), [World Link official](https://www.worldlinksarl.com/), [Coubeche official](https://coubeche.com/), [Al Gamil official](https://algamil.net/en/algamil), [Nougaprix official](https://algamil.net/nougaprix), [Casino Haramous Cybo](https://www.cybo.com/DJ-biz/casino), [Cash Center Petit Futé](https://www.petitfute.com/v51482-djibouti/c650-produits-gourmands-vins/c1080-grande-surface-generaliste/251135-cash-center.html), [Xuefeng Volza](https://www.volza.com/company-profile/xuefeng-import-export-sarl-95449042/), [Nimsam Kashi NBD](https://en.nbd.ltd/trader/info/NBDD3Y535368451), [Kheyredin Trading Volza](https://www.volza.com/company-profile/kheyredin-trading-company-106414240/), [Horn & Holland Trademo](https://www.trademo.com/companies/horn-holland-perishable-services-sas/50014133), [Baraka Vegetables & Fruits ODPIC](https://odpic.dj/publication-registre/baraka-vegetables-fruits-sarl/), [Djibouti Fresh official](https://www.djiboutifresh.com/en/about-us/), [Djibouti Fresh contact](https://www.djiboutifresh.com/contact-2/), [Share Gelleh Volza](https://www.volza.com/company-profile/share-gelleh-fruits-vegetables-2074068/), [Djibah Seafood official](https://djibahseafood.com/), [Djibah Seafood contact](https://djibahseafood.com/index.php/contact/), [Belayab Foods history](https://belayabfoods.com/history), [Supermarché de Djibouti SMD ODPIC](https://odpic.dj/publication-registre/supermarche-de-djibouti-smd/)
Signal quality: Medium-high for Djibouti route-to-market mapping because public official sources identify contactable importer-distributors, FMCG wholesalers, supermarket groups, cold-chain processors, QSR/hotel buyers and fresh-produce platforms; medium for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because the best named garlic buyers are public shipment-data targets with weak direct contactability.
Interpretation note: WITS reports Djibouti 2023 imports of fresh/chilled garlic at USD 754.18K and 2,145,930 kg, with Ethiopia supplying nearly all volume. Volza public snippets report 197 garlic buyers and 1,703 garlic shipments, but several named buyers are individual or shipment-profile records without official websites. Treat BMMI, World Link, Coubeche and Al Gamil as contactable route-to-market gates; treat Xuefeng, Nimsam, Kheyredin and Horn & Holland as paid-data discovery targets.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | BMMI Djibouti / World Link / Coubeche Distribution / Groupe Al Gamil | Core import-distribution and retail cluster. BMMI has the strongest official contactable profile through food-and-beverage wholesale/distribution, global sourcing, warehousing and a Djibouti Free Zone office. World Link adds official FMCG food import-export, wholesale and warehousing. Coubeche adds cold-chain food distribution and supermarket banners. Al Gamil adds hypermarket, fresh-produce and frozen-food scale plus public food/vegetable shipment signals. |
| 2 | Xuefeng Import Export / Nimsam Kashi / Kheyredin Trading / Horn & Holland Perishable Services | Public garlic and fresh-produce shipment cluster. Xuefeng, Nimsam and Kheyredin have the clearest public garlic or HS 070320 adjacency, while Horn & Holland validates scaled fresh-produce imports from Ethiopia. These should be the first names tested in paid customs data. |
| 3 | Nougaprix / Casino Haramous / Cash Center / Djibouti Fresh / Share Gelleh / SMD | Supermarket and fresh-produce demand cluster. These sources validate retail grocery, frozen-food, fruit-and-vegetable, online delivery and supermarket demand. Use them for distributor referrals and demand checks rather than assuming direct import ownership. |
| 4 | Djibah Seafood / Belayab Foods-Djibouti / Ayla Grand / Chicking / DWO AGRO | Frozen, QSR, hospitality and processor cluster. These leads support peeled refrigerated, frozen/IQF, ingredient or foodservice garlic validation, but most are downstream buyers rather than FCL fresh-garlic importers. |
| 5 | Public trade-data reconciliation | WITS and Volza agree that Djibouti has real garlic import volume, but public buyer names need reconciliation because Ethiopia-origin trade may be handled by border traders, produce importers, supermarket groups or mixed-container distributors. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs should test HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF and dehydrated/minced garlic keywords against BMMI, World Link, Coubeche, Al Gamil, Nougaprix, Xuefeng, Nimsam, Kheyredin, Horn & Holland, Djibouti Fresh and supermarket/QSR channels before quoting FCL volume. |

| 4 | Taylor Pacific-Stop & Shop / RH Trading / TST / Raumai / Waterfront Foodworld / Boroko Foodworld | Supermarket and cash-and-carry demand cluster. Taylor Pacific now controls the Stop & Shop supermarket network after the 2025 acquisition. RH, TST, Raumai, Waterfront and Boroko Foodworld validate modern retail, imported grocery, fresh-food, wholesale, cash-and-carry and fresh-produce demand across Port Moresby and Lae. |
| 5 | Public trade-data reconciliation | WITS direct-import and partner-export values differ materially, with China dominating the route and Australia/New Zealand contributing smaller flows. Treat the public numbers as directional and use them to seed paid customs searches, not as proof that any one public distributor owns the cargo. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs should test BNG, Food Pro, SVS, AD Commodities, Pacific Industries, Rabtrad, Seeto Kui, NKW Fresh, Tininga, TST, RH, Taylor Pacific/Stop & Shop, Raumai, Waterfront Foodworld and processors across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF and dehydrated/minced garlic keywords before quoting FCL volume. |
