official](https://surgiprox.com/), [Volza Gabon fresh/mixed/vegetable buyer summary](https://www.volza.com/p/fresh-or-mixed-or-vegetable/buyers/buyers-in-gabon/)
Signal quality: Medium-high for Gabon route-to-market mapping because public sources identify contactable importers, supermarket groups, frozen/cold-chain distributors, FMCG wholesalers, cash-and-carry outlets and foodservice buyers; medium for beneficial garlic importer ownership because the best public garlic-adjacent shipment signal is still from paid-data snippets rather than official company garlic pages.
Interpretation note: WITS 2023 direct data reports Gabon fresh/chilled garlic imports of USD 608.36K and 1,626,670 kg, led by China at USD 577.38K and 1,618,190 kg. The legacy pipeline value is higher at USD 1.17M, so Gabon should be treated as a real container-capable garlic market but not quoted at FCL scale until paid customs data confirms the importer behind China-led HS 070320 entries.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Prix Import / SUPERGEL / SANgel / SIPAGEL | Core import, wholesale and cold-chain cluster. Prix Import is strongest for first outreach because public evidence combines direct import, wholesale, supermarkets, 10,000 m2 warehousing, fresh/dry food coverage and container sourcing. SUPERGEL, SANgel and SIPAGEL add frozen-food, refrigerated, cold-chain, institutional and frozen/IQF garlic routes. |
| 2 | CECA-GADIS / CEDICOM / FOBERD / GAREP / HM Distribution | National distribution and broadline wholesale cluster. CECA-GADIS validates 104-store retail scale and a 20,000 m2 logistics base. CEDICOM validates 30,000 m2 of warehouse space, 35 trucks and FMCG food-and-beverage wholesale. FOBERD adds food, frozen, condiments, logistics and agro-industrial routes. GAREP and HM Distribution add import-distribution and food-products wholesale coverage. |
| 3 | Resto Group / CMB / Servair Gabon / Hotel Adagio | Foodservice, QSR and institutional demand cluster. Resto Group adds PAUL, KFC, Pizza Hut, Sushi Bar, Pinkberry and central-kitchen/warehouse signals. CMB, Servair and Hotel Adagio validate hotel, airport-catering, restaurant, collectivity and event foodservice demand. |
| 4 | CECA-GADIS banners / REGABON / SIGALLI / Uncle Sam / GABFOOD / Sundeep / Surgiprox | Supermarket, cash-and-carry and supporting distributor cluster. These sources broaden demand coverage across grocery wholesale, superettes, frozen-food retail, cash-and-carry, dairy/beverage distribution and smaller local distributors, but they are weaker for direct FCL import ownership. |
| 5 | Public shipment-signal reconciliation | Public Volza snippets identify SIPAGEL, Resto Group BP and Royal Gel Gabon among high-volume Gabon fresh/mixed/vegetable buyers. SIPAGEL and Resto Group are contactable from public sources; Royal Gel remains a paid-data target because public records do not expose enough contactable official-source detail. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs should test Prix Import, SUPERGEL, SANgel, SIPAGEL, CECA-GADIS, CEDICOM, FOBERD, GAREP, HM Distribution, REGABON, Resto Group and Royal Gel across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF and dehydrated/minced garlic keywords before quoting FCL volume. |

Signal quality: Medium-high for Zimbabwe route-to-market mapping because public sources identify multiple contactable fresh-produce distributors, wholesale chains, supermarket groups, FMCG distributors, processors, cold-chain operators and QSR foodservice buyers; low for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because public WITS direct fresh-garlic imports are tiny and do not reconcile with the legacy pipeline value.
Interpretation note: WITS 2023 direct data reports Zimbabwe fresh/chilled garlic imports of only USD 3.15K and 453 kg from South Africa, while South Africa 2024 mirror-export data reports USD 4.05K and 841 kg to Zimbabwe. The legacy pipeline value of USD 1.11M is therefore not supported by public HS 070320 data and should be treated as an unresolved assumption until paid customs data checks informal cross-border, local garlic production, processed garlic, dehydrated garlic and mixed-grocery lanes.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Freshtrade / CF Produce / Morefresh | Fresh-produce cluster. Freshtrade is the strongest contactable route because official evidence combines 400 tons of produce moved monthly, warehousing, distribution, buyer-account management and regional importation where local product is unavailable. CF Produce and Morefresh add national retail-chain vegetable supply, foodservice produce-wholesale and nationwide fresh-produce coverage. |
| 2 | Moldon / N. Richards / Route to Market / Mega Save / GAIN | Wholesale, broadline distribution and cold-chain cluster. Moldon covers HORECA, foodservice and retail channels with 5,000-plus products. N. Richards covers food and cold-chain products through 30 branches. Route to Market covers more than 400 outlets, countrywide distribution and cold-chain warehousing. Mega Save and GAIN add cash-and-carry, fresh/frozen and institutional buyer routes. |
| 3 | OK Zimbabwe / TM Pick n Pay / SPAR / Food World | Supermarket demand cluster. OK Zimbabwe, TM Pick n Pay, SPAR and Food World collectively validate national retail demand for groceries, fruit and vegetables, fresh/frozen foods, wholesale branches and South Africa-linked sourcing. These are procurement-referral and demand-validation targets rather than confirmed fresh-garlic import owners. |
| 4 | National Foods / Cairns Foods / Probrands / Mambo / Food Discount Center / SM Distributors | Processor, ingredient and FMCG-distribution cluster. National Foods, Cairns and Probrands validate processed-food, sauce, condiment, grocery and FMCG channels. Mambo and Food Discount Center validate bulk food commodities, regional/international sourcing and processor supply. SM Distributors validates grocery and food-and-beverage route-to-market coverage. |
| 5 | Zenith Distributors / Simbisa Brands Zimbabwe | Frozen and foodservice cluster. Zenith validates cold-chain wholesale, frozen/chilled foods, imported fish and food-additive imports. Simbisa validates QSR scale across Chicken Inn, Pizza Inn, Nando's, Steers, Fish Inn, RocoMamas and other brands. Use this cluster for peeled refrigerated, frozen/IQF, minced or foodservice garlic validation. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should first reconcile whether Zimbabwe's legacy garlic value reflects local/informal supply, broader processed garlic, dehydrated garlic, garlic powder, mixed grocery imports or stale source data. Test Freshtrade, N. Richards, Route to Market, GAIN, Mega Save, OK, TM Pick n Pay, SPAR, Food World, Moldon, Mambo, National Foods, Cairns, Probrands, Zenith and Simbisa across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF, dehydrated/minced and garlic powder keywords before quoting FCL volume. |

## Fresh Garlic Import, Distribution, Wholesale, Supermarket And Processor Buyer Signal - Kyrgyz Republic

Source: [WITS Kyrgyz Republic fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/KGZ/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/ALL/product/070320), [WITS China exports to Kyrgyz Republic](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/All/year/2023/tradeflow/Exports/partner/KGZ/product/070320), [Volza Kyrgyzstan garlic imports](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic/import/import-in-kyrgyzstan/), [SB Group food products](https://sb.kg/food-products/), [Alliance Product official](https://alliance.kg/en), [Globus supplier contacts](https://globus.kg/en/suppliers-and-partners/we-have-buyers/staff-contacts), [Asia Retail supplier page](https://asia.kg/ru/postavka-produkcii/), [Asia Retail about page](https://asia.kg/ru/o-seti-magazinov-aziya/), [Kyrgyz Product garlic page](https://www.productkg.com/en/catalog/garlic-0), [VitaMiks official](https://vitamiks.kg/en/), [Prodexpo ABADA Trade fresh vegetables/garlic profile](https://www.prod-expo.ru/en/ci/20146/), [Prod Impex contact](https://prodimpex-sonun.com/en/contacts/), [MK Trade F&B profile](https://fb.kg/view/157), [Ejoo Trade official](https://ejoo.kg/), [Gastronom.kg public profile](https://kg.orgpage.ru/bishkek/gastronom-kg-5420879.html), [Seiyitbekov KFH public profile](https://flagma-kg.com/ru/843825/), [Bai Elim Central Asia Trade Forum profile](https://catradeforum.org/food-industry/), [Pamir/OSKO ITE profile](https://catalogue.ite-expo.ru/en-GB/new-Interactive-plan/exhibitor_view.aspx?project_id=526&stand_id=A1084), [Nuristan processed vegetables profile](https://2021.catradeforum.org/processed-fruit-and-vegetables/), [Advantex/Oimo profile](https://2020.catradeforum.org/processed-fruit-and-vegetables/), [Toyboss official](https://www.toyboss.kg/en/), [Akchabar Kyrgyz garlic production](https://www.akchabar.kg/en/news/urozhaj-chesnoka-po-sravneniyu-s-proshlim-godom-sobrali-na-20-tisyach-tonn-bolshe-lbouuzelfkxpucss)
Signal quality: Medium-high for Kyrgyz Republic route-to-market mapping because public sources identify contactable food importers, national distributors, supermarket procurement teams, HoReCa suppliers, direct dried-garlic exporters, frozen/IQF processors, canned-garlic processors and food manufacturers; low-medium for beneficial imported fresh-garlic ownership because public WITS direct importer data is much smaller than the legacy pipeline value and China mirror-export data.
Interpretation note: WITS 2023 direct importer data reports Kyrgyz Republic fresh/chilled garlic imports of USD 26.93K and 127,590 kg, while China mirror-export data reports USD 333.22K and 327,385 kg to Kyrgyz Republic. Volza public snippets report 667 Kyrgyzstan garlic import shipments across 7 importers and 9 suppliers, but those public snippets do not expose enough contact-ready buyer detail. Treat the market as real but mixed between local production, regional trade, China-origin imports and processed/dried garlic channels.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | SB Group / Alliance Product / MK Trade / Seiyitbekov KFH | Core import-distribution and fresh-produce trader cluster. SB Group is strongest because official pages show imported food products, fruits and vegetables from Europe, Asia, the USA, China, Turkey and Egypt, own-company customs clearance, warehousing, logistics and perishable-cargo handling. Alliance Product adds national FMCG distribution, wholesale, retail and HoReCa scale. MK Trade and Seiyitbekov add public food-distribution and fruit/vegetable import-export routes. |
| 2 | Globus / Asia Retail / Gastronom.kg / Zakup Express / Anar Market | Supermarket and HoReCa demand cluster. Globus provides a named fresh produce category manager, while Asia Retail publishes supplier intake rules and supplier email for a 59-store Asia/Azbuka network. Gastronom.kg, Zakup Express and Anar Market validate HoReCa food-supply demand, useful for peeled refrigerated garlic, foodservice packs and distributor referrals. |
| 3 | Kyrgyz Product / VitaMiks / ABADA Trade / Prod Impex / Bai Elim / Pamir / Nuristan | Direct garlic, frozen/IQF and processing cluster. Kyrgyz Product has direct dried-garlic evidence; VitaMiks validates IQF frozen vegetables and refrigerated logistics; ABADA Trade has public garlic and garlic-product exhibition evidence; Nuristan has direct canned-garlic and sterilized frozen vegetable evidence; Prod Impex, Bai Elim and Pamir validate scaled canned, pickled, sauce and processed-vegetable platforms. |
| 4 | Ejoo Trade / OSKO / Advantex-Oimo / Toyboss | Ingredient, dehydrated and food-manufacturing cluster. Ejoo supplies food ingredients to producers and HoReCa; OSKO validates vacuum-dried B2B ingredients; Advantex/Oimo validates processed-food technology and export channels; Toyboss validates high-volume food manufacturing and garlic-seasoned product demand. Use this cluster for dehydrated, minced, seasoning, sauce and ingredient garlic formats. |
| 5 | Public trade-data reconciliation | Public direct-import values are much smaller than the legacy pipeline value and lower than China mirror-export data. Kyrgyz garlic production is also growing, so direct imported fresh-garlic demand may be seasonal or routed through mixed Central Asia border trade and supermarket/distributor consolidation. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid customs should test SB Group, Alliance Product, Globus/Narodnyi, Asia Retail, MK Trade, Seiyitbekov, Kyrgyz Product, VitaMiks, ABADA Trade, Prod Impex, Bai Elim, Pamir, Nuristan, Ejoo Trade and HoReCa suppliers across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF, dried/dehydrated, minced and garlic-powder keywords before quoting FCL volume. |

Signal quality: Medium-high for Mauritania route-to-market mapping because official and public business-directory sources identify contactable food importers, cold/dry/frozen distributors, fresh-produce distributors, commodity traders, agro-industrial processors, supermarkets, hotels and offshore export-consolidation routes; medium-low for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because WITS direct importer data, China mirror-export data and public shipment-sample evidence do not agree on market size or name the main beneficial importer.
Interpretation note: U.S. Commercial Service guidance says Mauritania imports are distributed from Nouakchott/Nouadhibou ports through wholesalers, distributors and retailers, with large wholesalers linked to family conglomerates accounting for much import trade. That matches the public-source buyer map: prioritize scaled import/distribution groups first, use supermarkets and hotels for demand/referrals, and escalate to paid data before quoting FCL volumes.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Gimex / Ejmel / MauriMarket / MOLIGOO | Core food-import and wholesale-distribution cluster. Gimex is strongest because official pages show import/distribution, cold/frozen/dry storage, fruit center, 3,000 products, supermarket supply and HoReCa coverage. Ejmel adds food-and-beverage distribution to catering, hotels, wholesalers and sub-distributors. MauriMarket and MOLIGOO add direct food-import and broad packaged-food import evidence. |
| 2 | Wafa Holding / Noor Holding / Weddady / General Mauritania Food | Agroindustry, group distribution and processor cluster. Wafa and Noor validate group-scale import-export, distribution, food processing, cold-chain or commercial representation. Weddady and General Mauritania Food add agroindustry and processing/packaging signals. This cluster is useful for processed, ingredient, peeled/minced, foodservice and paid-data screening rather than confirmed fresh-garlic import ownership. |
| 3 | MALAK Distribution / General Trade RIM / BICOG / CREP-Consulting | Fresh-produce and commodity-trade cluster. MALAK has the clearest fruits/vegetables distribution evidence. General Trade RIM, BICOG and CREP validate food-product commerce, food commodities and supplier representation. These are practical paid-data targets for testing who owns HS 070320 flows. |
| 4 | Atac El Khair / Supermarche Salam / Mauri Center | Supermarket demand cluster. These Nouakchott grocery/supermarket sources validate modern retail demand, frozen goods, cooking supplies, food-and-beverage retail and wholesale-commerce signals. Use them for procurement referrals and landed retail price checks. |
| 5 | Sheraton / FASQ / Azalai / AFCO / Miamland | Foodservice, cold-chain and consolidation cluster. Sheraton, FASQ and Azalai validate hotel, catering, event and restaurant demand. AFCO validates industrial freezing and food-processing infrastructure. Miamland validates an offshore French export-wholesale route for Mauritania-bound fresh/frozen/dry products. |
| 6 | Public trade-data reconciliation and paid customs target discovery | WITS direct importer data reports USD 205.56K and 1.84M kg in 2023, while China mirror-export data reports USD 2.07M and 1.57M kg to Mauritania. Public CIC Trade Pro sample data exposes a Med El Hacen fresh/chilled garlic shipment from China to Nouakchott in 2024. Paid customs data should test Gimex, Ejmel, MauriMarket, MOLIGOO, MALAK, Wafa, Noor, General Trade RIM, BICOG, Atac, Supermarche Salam and Med El Hacen across HS 070320, peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF and dried/minced/garlic-powder keywords before quoting FCL volume. |

Signal quality: High for Equatorial Guinea route-to-market mapping because public official and directory sources identify scaled supermarket/wholesale groups, cold-chain distributors, fresh/frozen/dry food distributors, online supermarket demand, food processors, retail fresh-produce demand and major hotel/conference foodservice buyers; medium-low for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because public garlic trade data is thin and inconsistent with the legacy pipeline value.
Interpretation note: The best public outreach sequence should start with national distribution groups, not hotels or small supermarkets. Public hotel and supermarket demand is useful for referrals and format validation, but paid customs data is needed to identify whether garlic is consolidated through Martinez Hermanos, EGTC, Santy, GUINACO, Alpha, LUMECO, Spanish/French consolidators, China-origin mixed containers or regional Cameroon/Gabon flows.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Martinez Hermanos / EGTC / Comercial Santy / GUINACO | Core supermarket, wholesale and cold-chain distribution cluster. Martinez Hermanos is now the strongest contactable lead because official sources show major wholesale/retail distribution, main-distributor language, countrywide reach, supermarket network and cold-chain frozen infrastructure. EGTC, Santy and GUINACO add fresh/frozen foods, B2B distribution, container logistics, hotel/restaurant/collectivity supply and national/regional coverage. |
| 2 | Alpha Group / LUMECO / Miamland / Situcka | Dry/frozen import, cold-chain and export-consolidation cluster. Alpha Group provides official dry and frozen food import/distribution evidence. LUMECO validates import and wholesale/retail sale of frozen products in Malabo and Bata. Miamland adds offshore fresh/frozen/dry export consolidation. Situcka validates local online supermarket demand and supplier mapping. |
| 3 | Hache Alimentaria / Supermercado Manolo / Tienda Ideal / SOLFAGE / La Sabrosa | Processor, supermarket and fresh-produce demand cluster. Hache validates local food-product processing and retail distribution through Martinez, EGTC, Lumeco, Situcka and others. The smaller supermarket/fresh-produce names validate retail demand and distributor-referral routes, not direct FCL import ownership. |
| 4 | Sofitel / Colinas / Grand Hotel Djibloho / Hotel Anda / ibis / Bisila Palace | Hotel, resort and conference foodservice cluster. These official hotel sources validate restaurant, buffet, bar, meeting, event, catering and full-board dining demand. Use them for peeled refrigerated, minced, prepared and foodservice garlic validation through local distributors. |
| 5 | Public trade-data reconciliation | WITS 2019 mirror-export data showed China-led fresh/chilled garlic exports to Equatorial Guinea of USD 281.50K and 248,500 kg plus smaller EU/Spain flows. WITS 2023 EU mirror exports show only USD 17.73K and 4,422 kg, while 2023 onion/shallot mirror exports to Equatorial Guinea are materially larger. Treat the legacy pipeline value as unverified until paid data reconciles direct imports, mirror exports and mixed-container routes. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should test Martinez Hermanos, EGTC, Comercial Santy, GUINACO, Alpha Group, LUMECO, Ecua Market, Miamland, Situcka, Hache Alimentaria, Supermercado Manolo, Tienda Ideal, SOLFAGE, La Sabrosa and major hotel groups across HS 070320, peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF and dried/minced/garlic-powder keywords before quoting full-container volume. |

| 4 | Mini Mercado Matilde / Calu & Angela / Boutique dos Congelados / Meu Super / Supermercado Almada Tavares | Supermarket and frozen-food demand cluster. | 5 | Hilton Cabo Verde / ROBINSON Cabo Verde / Oasis Atlantico Cabo Verde Hotels / Ki-Bom | Resort, hotel and professional foodservice cluster. Hilton, ROBINSON and Oasis validate restaurant, buffet, room-service, bar, meeting, event and multi-property hotel demand across the tourism islands. | Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | CITC Food Group / Prime Foods / Paradise Supplies | Core importer, wholesaler, supermarket and foodservice distribution cluster. Use this cluster for retail checks and distributor referrals, not direct FCL ownership unless customs data proves otherwise. |
| 3 | Miamland | Offshore consolidation cluster. Miamland validates a France-based export-wholesale route for fresh, frozen and dry products to Cook Islands, serving mass retail, foodservice and hospitality buyers. This is useful if smaller island buyers need consolidation, but it is not a local beneficial importer. |
| 4 | Pacific Resort / The Rarotongan / Edgewater / Crown / Nautilus | Major resort and events foodservice cluster. These official hotel sources validate restaurants, bars, buffet, room service, weddings, meetings, private dining, corporate functions and event foodservice across Rarotonga and Aitutaki. Use this cluster for peeled refrigerated, minced, prepared and fresh garlic demand through distributors. |
| 5 | Aitutaki Lagoon / Muri Beach Club / Manuia-OTB / Tamanu / Tamarind / Trader Jacks | Secondary hospitality and restaurant demand cluster. These sources broaden foodservice coverage through resort restaurants, beach bars, island nights, weddings, corporate events, seafood restaurants and fresh Pacific cuisine. They are downstream validators and referral targets, not first-pass FCL import owners. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should test CITC, Prime Foods, Paradise Supplies, Wigmore's, Maina Traders, Miamland, Super Brown, Tina & Co, Aquila and resort procurement channels across HS 070320, peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF and dried/minced/garlic-powder keywords before quoting full-container volume. |

Official-source review 2026-05-02: use Prime Foods Ltd as the current top contact-ready official Cook Islands outreach lead because official Prime Foods pages validate wholesale ordering, produce, meat and dry-goods supply, quality local and international food sourcing, business details for Prime Foods Ltd, direct sales email and Rarotonga phone/location details. Keep CITC Food Group / Cook Islands Trading Corporation as the stronger public paid-customs/FCL ownership target because public professional and business profiles validate importer, distributor, wholesaler and retailer positioning, five Rarotonga food stores, the largest supermarket/food outlet in the Cook Islands, retail/wholesale food coverage and distribution across hospitality, trade and outer islands, while Volza lists Cook Islands Trading Corporation as an importer with 242 shipments. Paradise Supplies remains the cleanest official hospitality-wholesale route for hotels, resorts and cafes.

Signal quality: High for Brunei route-to-market mapping because public WITS data shows a container-capable garlic market and official company pages identify contactable food importers, distributors, wholesalers, cold-chain operators, supermarkets, halal food processors and institutional caterers. Medium for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because public sources identify routes and some direct garlic buyer evidence, but do not prove which companies booked HS 070320 shipments.
Interpretation note: Brunei's best public outreach sequence should start with Bukit Ladang, Ying Bee, TGT, Sullivans, GHL, MC Maju, Teeyem and Ben Foods before moving to supermarkets, directory-only buyers or institutional foodservice. Public buyer-directory and Volza names are useful for paid-data matching, but direct outreach should prioritize official-source companies with phone/email and distributor infrastructure.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Bukit Ladang / Ying Bee / Lee & Soh / TGT | Core garlic import, distribution and cold-chain cluster. Bukit Ladang is strongest because official pages state fresh-commodity imports including garlic, onions, ginger, potatoes and carrots plus distribution to mini-markets and supermarkets. Ying Bee adds over 400 retailers, over 300 HoReCA customers and chilled/frozen logistics. Lee & Soh adds public fresh-garlic buyer and onion/garlic shipment signals, while TGT adds official major importer/wholesaler status and cold storage for 200 forty-foot containers. |
| 2 | Sullivans / GHL / MC Maju / Teeyem / Ben Foods | Broadline food distribution cluster. Sullivans, GHL, MC Maju, Teeyem and Ben Foods validate chilled/frozen, imported fresh, herbs/spices, halal cold-chain, FMCG, wholesale and foodservice routes. This group should be tested for fresh whole, peeled refrigerated and frozen/IQF garlic, with paid customs data needed to determine beneficial importer ownership. |
| 3 | Supa Save / Sifana / Double M / Sunshine | Supermarket, wholesale and retail-distribution cluster. Supa Save validates imported grocery, fresh-produce and frozen-food retail demand. Sifana and Sunshine validate broad supermarket, mini-market, restaurant and wholesaler customer networks. Double M validates Asian food import/wholesale plus public garlic pickle/garlic shipment snippets. |
| 4 | WNF / Al Salam / Thaamin | Public fresh-garlic buyer-directory cluster. Go4WorldBusiness lists WNF, Al Salam and Thaamin as Brunei fresh-garlic or fresh-vegetable buyers, but no official websites or direct public contacts were verified. Use them for paid-data matching, directory messaging and buyer identity reconciliation, not immediate cold outreach. |
| 5 | Ghanim / Hua Ho / Soon Lee / Royal Brunei Culinary | Processor, supermarket and institutional foodservice cluster. Ghanim validates halal food trading, ready-to-eat and frozen-product routes. Hua Ho and Soon Lee validate supermarket and fresh-produce demand. Royal Brunei Culinary validates central-kitchen, aviation, healthcare, catering, restaurant and cafe demand. This cluster is better for peeled refrigerated, minced, frozen/IQF and foodservice garlic validation than first-pass FCL fresh whole ownership. |
| 6 | Public trade-data reconciliation and paid customs target discovery | WITS 2023 reports Brunei fresh/chilled garlic imports of USD 2.32M and 2.71M kg, led by China, India, Malaysia, Netherlands and Pakistan. Paid customs data should test Bukit Ladang, Ying Bee, Lee & Soh, TGT, Sullivans, GHL, MC Maju, Teeyem, Ben Foods, Supa Save, Sifana, Double M, WNF, Al Salam, Thaamin, Sunshine, Ghanim, Hua Ho, Soon Lee and RBC across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF and dried/minced/garlic-powder keywords before quoting full-container volume. |

## Fresh Garlic Import, Distribution, Wholesale, Supermarket And Foodservice Buyer Signal - Sao Tome and Principe

Source: [WITS Sao Tome and Principe fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/STP/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/ALL/product/070320), [CECA-GADIS Super CKDO](https://cecagadis.com/enseigne-super-ckdo/), [Volza Sao Tome and Principe garlic buyers](https://www.volza.com/p/garlic/buyers/buyers-in-sao-tome-and-principe/), [Volza Doce profile](https://www.volza.com/company-profile/doce-unipersonal-ltd-90009147/), [Volza Continental profile](https://www.volza.com/company-profile/continental-supermarket-ltd.-100435404/), [TradeAtlas Vital](https://www.tradeatlas.com/en/firms/vital-lda/801d24eb8ce5cca9), [Miamland Sao Tome and Principe](https://www.miamland.com/wholesaler-supplier-country-sao-tome-et-principe/), [Intermar](https://www.africabizinfo.com/pt-ST/intermar-lda-222-1250), [Mini Mercado Bom Preco](https://www.africabizinfo.com/pt-ST/bom-pre%C3%A7o-222-3421), [Supermercado Coconote](https://www.africabizinfo.com/pt-ST/supermercado-coconote-222-6031), [Cash do Equador](https://listing.elidge.com/listing/cash-do-equador/), [Pingo Doxi](https://paginasamarelas.cybo.com/ST-biz/pingo-doxi), [Aegis Trading Sao Tome](https://saotomefish.com/), [Volza Carla Charcutaria](https://www.volza.com/company-profile/carla-charcutaria-ltd.-110736290/), [Volza Sao Tome sea/food buyers](https://www.volza.com/p/sea-or-food/buyers/buyers-in-sao-tome-and-principe/), [Principe Collection Omali dining](https://www.principecollection.com/en/hotels/omali/dining), [Pestana Sao Tome](https://www.pestana.com/en/hotel/pestana-sao-tome), [Hotel Praia restaurant](https://www.hotel-praia.com/restaurante/), [Club Santana](https://www.clubsantana.com/en/le-club), [Praia Inhame contacts](https://www.hotelpraiainhame.com/en/hotel-contacts.html)
Signal quality: Medium for Sao Tome and Principe route-to-market mapping because public sources identify one contactable supermarket/import lead with garlic-buyer evidence, several shipment-signal importers, supermarket/grocery demand, offshore export-wholesale consolidation, cold-chain infrastructure and hotel/resort foodservice demand; low-medium for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because most local importer names beyond CECA/Super CKDO are shipment-profile or directory-backed rather than official contact-ready sources.
Interpretation note: WITS 2023 reports Sao Tome and Principe fresh/chilled garlic imports of USD 191.18K and 140,235 kg. This supports real demand but is smaller than the legacy pipeline value, so paid customs intelligence should reconcile direct imports, mirror exports and mixed-container grocery shipments before assigning FCL ownership.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | CECA GADIS STP LDA / Super CKDO / Afrimar | Core garlic-buyer and food-import cluster. CECA/Super CKDO is strongest because official CECA-GADIS pages show the Super CKDO banner in Sao Tome with fresh, local, frozen, dairy and other food products, while Volza publicly names CECA GADIS STP LDA/STP LTD among leading garlic importers. Afrimar adds public garlic-buyer and broader milk, dried fruit, chicken and vegetable import signals, but no official website or public direct contact was verified. |
| 2 | Vital / Doce / Continental Supermarket | Shipment-backed food import and supermarket cluster. Vital has public food-product and milk/animal-feed import signals, Doce has public frozen chicken, pasta, pulses, wheat flour and mixed-food shipment evidence, and Continental has public supermarket import evidence. These are strong paid-data matching targets but should not be treated as contact-ready garlic importers until official contacts or customs records are verified. |
| 3 | Miamland / Intermar / Mini Mercado Bom Preco / Coconote / Cash do Equador / Pingo Doxi | Offshore consolidation and local grocery-demand cluster. Miamland validates a France-based export-wholesale route for fresh, frozen, grocery and foodservice products to Sao Tome and Principe. Intermar, Bom Preco, Coconote, Cash do Equador and Pingo Doxi validate local supermarket and grocery demand, useful for distributor referrals and landed-price checks rather than direct FCL ownership. |
| 4 | Aegis / Carla Charcutaria / Karley Martins Lima / CS Trading | Cold-chain, processor and shipment-signal cluster. Aegis validates cold rooms, freezing tunnels, fresh/frozen food infrastructure and processor-adjacent cold chain. Carla Charcutaria has public chicken, tomato and preserved-food import evidence. Karley Martins Lima and CS Trading appear in public sea/food buyer snippets and are paid-data discovery targets for mixed-food channels. |
| 5 | Principe Collection / Pestana / Hotel Praia / Club Santana / Praia Inhame | Hotel, resort and foodservice demand cluster. These official hospitality sources validate restaurant, bar, buffet, event, banquet, resort-kitchen and lodge-restaurant demand. Use them for peeled refrigerated, minced, prepared and fresh garlic demand through local distributors, not as first-pass FCL import owners. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should test CECA/Super CKDO, Afrimar, Vital, Doce, Continental, Miamland, Intermar, Bom Preco, Coconote, Cash do Equador, Pingo Doxi, Aegis, Carla Charcutaria, Karley Martins Lima, CS Trading and major hotel groups across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF, dried/dehydrated, minced and garlic-powder keywords before quoting full-container volume. |

Signal quality: Medium-high for Zambia route-to-market mapping because public sources identify contactable cold-chain importer-distributors, national FMCG distributors, fresh-produce wholesalers, central supermarket distribution, imported ingredient suppliers, processors, prepared-food manufacturers, ethnic grocery channels and multi-brand restaurant demand; medium-low for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because public garlic trade data is smaller than the legacy pipeline value and public sources do not identify the companies booking HS 070320 containers.
Interpretation note: WITS 2023 reports Zambia fresh/chilled garlic imports of USD 178.61K and 373,913 kg, led by China, South Africa and India, while mirror-export data reports China, South Africa and Malawi supplying USD 222.84K and 221,977 kg. Treat Zambia as a real but customs-reconciliation-dependent market: prioritize scaled distributor and fresh-produce channels, then use supermarkets and foodservice groups for procurement referrals and demand validation.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | EPIC Foods / Gatbro / Ntsele-Fruit & Veg City / Choppies / Unity | Core importer-distributor, fresh-produce and supermarket cluster. EPIC is strongest because official pages show importation, distribution, frozen/chilled storage and a multi-temperature fleet. Gatbro adds national FMCG distribution scale and 10 warehouses. Ntsele/Fruit & Veg City adds fresh/frozen produce, cold storage, fast-service restaurant, hotel, retail, mining and hospitality supply. Choppies adds supermarket distribution-centre coverage. Unity adds Copperbelt FMCG, pasta/dry grocery, supermarket and wholesale coverage. |
| 2 | Artion / Zambeef / Miamland / Dew Fresh / Zambezi Brands | Foodservice, cold-chain and imported-ingredient cluster. Artion validates baking/catering ingredients, spices, sauces, customs/forwarding and supermarket/hotel/restaurant customers. Zambeef validates national cold-chain logistics, retail, wholesale and processor demand. Miamland validates an offshore fresh/frozen/grocery consolidation route. Dew Fresh validates local/imported fresh and frozen produce for tourism/hospitality. Zambezi Brands validates imported international ingredient supply to foodservice professionals. |
| 3 | Gammer / Kwenu Kwetu / Trade Kings / Retsol | Processor, seasoning and prepared-food cluster. Gammer is strongest for food-grade ingredients used by bakeries, meat processors, dairies, sauces and salad-dressing manufacturers. Kwenu Kwetu validates bulk fresh vegetables, groceries and processed foods for restaurants, hotels, caterers and retailers. Trade Kings validates condiments, seasoning and FMCG food manufacturing. Retsol validates prepared meals, deli, frozen bakery and retail-industry food manufacturing. |
| 4 | Pick n Pay / Shoprite / Food Lovers Market | Supermarket and fresh-produce demand cluster. Pick n Pay and Shoprite validate modern multi-store retail demand and price checks. Food Lovers Market adds a fresh-produce-heavy supermarket channel with vegetables, fruit, meat, fish, bakery and prepared food. Use these buyers for procurement referrals and landed-price intelligence, not direct FCL ownership unless customs data proves direct imports. |
| 5 | Oriental Food / Fortune Retail Holdings / Chimfresh | Ethnic grocery, restaurant group and institutional-fresh supply cluster. Oriental Food validates Asian sauces, spices, frozen food, fruit/vegetable and restaurant/hotel supply. Fortune validates large multi-brand restaurant demand for peeled, minced and fresh garlic. Chimfresh validates fresh produce, FMCG, aquaculture and scalable cold-chain distribution to retail chains and institutional buyers. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should test EPIC, Gatbro, Ntsele/Fruit & Veg City, Choppies, Unity, Artion, Zambeef, Dew Fresh, Miamland, Zambezi Brands, Gammer, Kwenu Kwetu, Trade Kings, Retsol, Pick n Pay, Shoprite, Food Lovers Market, Oriental Food, Fortune and Chimfresh across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF, dried/dehydrated, minced and garlic-powder keywords before quoting full-container volume. |

Source: [WITS global fresh/chilled garlic imports](https://wits.worldbank.org/trade/comtrade/en/country/ALL/year/2023/tradeflow/Imports/partner/WLD/product/070320), [Triple J divisions](https://triplej.net/about-us/divisions), [Triple J contact](https://triplej.net/contact-us), [K&K Island Pride](https://www.kkislandpride.com/), [Pacific Island Trade K&K contact](https://pacificislandtrade.com/pages/contact), [Marshall Islands Guide food stores](https://www.infomarshallislands.com/food/), [MISCO official](https://miscomarket.com/), [MISCO contact](https://miscomarket.com/contact-us), [MIMRA OIFMC page](https://www.rmimimra.com/about-us/coastal-community-affairs), [EZ Price Mart](https://mh.near-place.com/ez-price-mart-majuro/en), [Formosa Supermarket](https://mh.near-place.com/formosa-supermarket-marshall-islands), [H2 Imports Ebeye](https://www.cybo.com/MH-biz/h-imports-ebeye), [Surfway Grocery Store](https://evendo.com/locations/marshall-islands/kwajalein-atoll/restaurant/surfway-grocery-store), [Ebeye Mart](https://evendo.com/locations/marshall-islands/lib-island/bar/ebeye-mart), [Enra Restaurant](https://www.infomarshallislands.com/enra-restaurant/), [Marshall Islands Resort](https://www.majuromir.com/), [RRE Hotel](https://www.rreinc.com/rrehotel.html), [RRE reservations](https://www.rreinc.com/reservations.htm), [Toeak Bar & Grill](https://www.infomarshallislands.com/toeak-bar-grill/), [Flame Tree](https://www.infomarshallislands.com/flame-tree/), [Dar Cafe](https://www.infomarshallislands.com/dar-cafe/), [Won Hai Shien](https://www.infomarshallislands.com/won-hai-shien/)
Signal quality: Medium for Marshall Islands route-to-market mapping because public sources identify a contactable wholesale/grocery group, a major fresh-produce supermarket with wholesale manager evidence, wholesale divisions, a government-backed fish-market/cold-chain produce node, supermarkets, Asian grocery stores, Ebeye/Kwajalein retail demand and major hotel/restaurant foodservice demand; low-medium for beneficial fresh-garlic importer ownership because public WITS visibility is thin and likely affected by U.S./Guam consolidation, freight forwarding and mixed-container grocery procurement.
Interpretation note: Treat Marshall Islands as a small but commercially mapped island-grocery and foodservice market. Triple J, K&K, Pacific Basin Payless and MISCO are the strongest public route-to-market leads, but paid customs intelligence is required before assigning FCL ownership or quoting container-volume garlic programs.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Triple J Marshalls Inc. / Payless Triple J | Strongest official-source lead. Triple J's official divisions page states that its wholesale food distribution business operates across Micronesia, serves hotels, restaurants and retail grocery stores, has had a Marshall Islands retail grocery presence for more than 30 years, and that Triple J Marshalls manages wholesale and retail operations in Ebeye and Majuro, operates two retail grocery stores and distributes to other retailers. |
| 2 | K&K Island Pride / K&K Corporation / Pacific Basin Payless | Major fresh-produce and wholesale supermarket cluster. K&K's official page identifies a retail and wholesale store, Pacific Island Trade publishes a K&K wholesale manager contact, and Marshall Islands Guide says Island Pride and Payless are the two largest fresh-produce and general-food outlets in Majuro with weekly flown-in produce and monthly or twice-monthly shipped produce. The same guide says Island Pride and Pacific Basin Payless both have wholesale divisions. |
| 3 | MISCO / MIMRA OIFMC | Wholesale/retail and fresh-food cold-chain cluster. MISCO's official site identifies MISCO Wholesale & Retail and publishes a direct Marshall Islands contact number. MIMRA's official OIFMC page validates a Majuro fish-market center selling fresh fish, fish jerky, fillet and local produce, plus an Ebeye/Kwajalein fish-market node. This cluster supports fresh, frozen and foodservice demand validation, but not direct garlic FCL ownership without customs data. |
| 4 | EZ Price / Formosa / Cost Price / H2 Imports / Surfway / Ebeye Mart | Supermarket and local grocery-demand cluster. EZ Price, Formosa and Cost Price validate Majuro grocery and Asian supermarket demand, while H2 Imports, Surfway and Ebeye Mart add Ebeye/Kwajalein retail and import-adjacent coverage. Use this group for route mapping, distributor referrals and paid-data name matching. |
| 5 | Marshall Islands Resort/Enra / RRE-Tide Table / Toeak / Flame Tree / Dar Cafe / Won Hai Shien | Hotel, restaurant and downstream foodservice cluster. Public sources validate resort dining, private dining, hotel restaurants, event catering, western restaurants, cafes and Chinese/Asian kitchen demand. These buyers are better targets for peeled refrigerated, minced and prepared garlic demand through distributors than direct FCL fresh-garlic ownership. |
| 6 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should test Triple J Marshalls/Payless Triple J, K&K/K&K Corporation, Pacific Basin Payless, MISCO, MIMRA/OIFMC, EZ Price, Formosa, Cost Price, H2 Imports, Surfway, Ebeye Mart and major resort/restaurant buyers across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF, dried/dehydrated, minced and garlic-powder keywords before quoting full-container volume. |

Signal quality: Medium-high for Rwanda route-to-market mapping because public sources identify visible garlic/onion buyer names, imported fresh-produce platforms, nationwide food importers/distributors, FMCG wholesale routes, fresh-produce aggregators, restaurant/hotel suppliers, modern supermarket demand and large hotel/convention foodservice demand; medium for beneficial garlic importer ownership because several strongest allium names come from public shipment profiles rather than official company pages.
Interpretation note: Treat Rwanda as a landlocked customs-reconciliation market: prioritize XS Fresh and named allium shipment buyers for paid-data matching, then use contactable food distributors, fresh-produce aggregators, supermarket buyers and Kigali hotel foodservice for route mapping, landed-price validation and peeled/minced demand.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | XS Fresh / SM General Trading / Ntahomvukiye Anastase / New One Design | Direct garlic/onion and fresh-produce discovery cluster. XS Fresh is the best public lead because official pages validate imported fresh produce for Rwanda's wholesale/retail market and public shipment pages identify it among garlic/onion buyers. SM General Trading, Ntahomvukiye Anastase and New One Design are allium/fresh-produce paid-data matching targets, with weaker official contactability. |
| 2 | Prodi / Eri-Rwanda / Akagera Trading / ICON Trading | Contactable importer-distributor cluster. Prodi validates nationwide imported food and beverage wholesale distribution, Eri validates food and bakery product import/distribution, Akagera Trading validates multi-origin FMCG imports and regional wholesale distribution, and ICON validates wholesale/import/export, bulk food, fresh vegetables and fresh/frozen meat supply to wholesalers, supermarkets, hotels and distributors. |
| 3 | National Distributors Rwanda / Krishna Rwanda / Simba Supermarket | Public FMCG, food-wholesale and modern-retail cluster. National Distributors is listed as a Kigali FMCG and foodservice distributor, Krishna appears in public Rwanda food-wholesaler directories, and Simba validates modern supermarket grocery demand. Use these for local procurement referrals and distributor cross-checks rather than first-pass FCL ownership. |
| 4 | TCYield / Izere Services / Food Bundles / Aman Fresh | Fresh-produce and foodservice supply cluster. TCYield validates large farmer aggregation and bulk fresh-cut produce for supermarkets, restaurants and quick-service chains. Izere validates fresh fruits, vegetables, tubers, processing and institutional/hotel supply. Food Bundles and Aman Fresh validate restaurant, hotel and customized fresh-ingredient supply routes. |
| 5 | Garden Fresh / Africa Foods Supply | Fresh-produce production, processing and local supply cluster. Garden Fresh adds export-oriented fresh vegetable production and outgrower consolidation, while Africa Foods Supply adds fresh vegetables, fruits, grains, processing and farm-to-table activity. These are useful for local fresh/processed demand, not direct garlic import ownership without customs confirmation. |
| 6 | Kigali Serena / Kigali Marriott / Radisson Blu Kigali | Hotel, banquet and convention foodservice cluster. These official hotel sources validate restaurant, buffet, brunch, room-service, banquet and convention demand in Kigali. They are downstream targets for peeled refrigerated, minced and prepared garlic through distributors rather than first-pass FCL fresh-garlic importers. |
| 7 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should test XS Fresh, SM General Trading, Ntahomvukiye Anastase, New One Design, Prodi, Eri-Rwanda, TCYield, Akagera Trading, ICON Trading, National Distributors Rwanda, Krishna Rwanda, Izere Services, Food Bundles, Aman Fresh, Garden Fresh, Africa Foods Supply, Simba and major Kigali hotel buyers across HS 070320 plus peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF, dried/dehydrated, minced and garlic-powder keywords before quoting full-container volume. |

Interpretation note: Start with Seyal for contactable FCL route validation; use E T S Marche Max, Hadji Youssouf Djibrine, E T S Pro Market, Star Gold and ETS AMA for paid-data matching; use Miamland, MAYELBUSINESS, Noussahim, Avi Trene and TchadMarket for route checks; use supermarkets and hotels for demand/referral mapping.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Seyal-Tchad / E T S Marche Max / Hadji Youssouf Djibrine / E T S Pro Market SN / Star Gold / ETS AMA | Importer and processed-garlic paid-data cluster. Seyal validates direct food import/export and container handling. Volza/Trademo profiles expose processed/dehydrated garlic-powder or food-preparation buyer names that should be tested before fresh whole garlic quoting. |
| 2 | Miamland Chad / MAYELBUSINESS / TCHAD.NOUSSAHIM / Avi Trene Food / TchadMarket | Offshore consolidation, foodstuffs, fresh-produce and processor cluster. Miamland validates fresh/frozen/grocery export-wholesale to Chad, MAYELBUSINESS validates foodstuff trading, Noussahim validates fruit/vegetable activity, Avi Trene validates agro-food processing and TchadMarket validates local agroalimentaire marketplace/import-export positioning. |
| 3 | Lily's Supermarket / Modern Market / Le Bon Marche / Le GRAND MARCHE / Alimentation La Tchadienne | Supermarket and grocery-demand cluster. Lily's validates fresh produce and imported grocery demand. Modern Market, Le Bon Marche, Le GRAND MARCHE and Alimentation La Tchadienne validate N'Djamena grocery/supermarket channels for SKU checks and distributor referrals. |
| 4 | Radisson Blu / Ledger Plaza / Hotel La Residence / Hotel Le Process | Hotel and foodservice cluster. These hotels validate restaurants, bars, rooms, meetings, banquets and event-driven foodservice demand for fresh, peeled and prepared garlic. They should normally be reached through distributors rather than treated as direct FCL importers. |
| 5 | Paid customs-data target discovery | Paid data should test Seyal-Tchad, E T S Marche Max, Hadji Youssouf Djibrine, E T S Pro Market SN, Star Gold, ETS AMA, Miamland Chad, MAYELBUSINESS, TCHAD.NOUSSAHIM, Avi Trene Food, TchadMarket, Lily's, Modern Market, Le Bon Marche, Le GRAND MARCHE, Alimentation La Tchadienne, Radisson Blu, Ledger Plaza, Hotel La Residence and Hotel Le Process across HS 070320 plus garlic powder, dehydrated garlic, peeled/chilled, frozen/IQF, dried/minced, powder drinks, food preparations, onions and allium-adjacent HS 0703 keywords before quoting FCL volume. |

Interpretation note: Start with Miamland for official offshore consolidation and fresh/frozen/grocery route validation; use Bangui Mall/Bangui Market for official in-country grocery demand; use MARCHANDE EXPORT ET IMPORT, GROUP ALRACHID, BEN AHMED ALRACHID and AFRICA DISTRIBUTION for paid-data matching; use supermarkets, markets and hotels for SKU, demand and distributor-referral checks.

| Rank | Buyer | Public signal |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Miamland Central African Republic / Bangui Mall-Bangui Market | Official route and grocery-demand cluster. | 2 | MARCHANDE EXPORT ET IMPORT / GROUP ALRACHID / BEN AHMED ALRACHID / AFRICA DISTRIBUTION | Import-export and distribution cluster. | 4 | Rayan / Corail / Cedar Market / Marche Central / Prima / Leader / Starmarket / Park'N'shop / Supermarche Maya | Supermarket, central-market and grocery-demand cluster. Cybo and Evendo validate Bangui retail food channels, with Corail and Cedar Market adding explicit fresh-produce and grocery-essential evidence. | 5 | Paid customs-data target discovery | WITS 2024 mirror-export data confirms about USD 141.99K and 47,654 kg of fresh/chilled garlic exports to Central African Republic, mostly from Spain and the wider European Union. Baron validates sauces, condiments, spices, flavorings and spice-blend manufacturing in St. Lucia. Abby's validates wholesale/retail manufactured natural food products and snacks. Inter-Island validates foodservice/catering supply relationships. These are processed-garlic, ingredient, packaging/referral or paid-data matching targets. |
| 4 | Coconut Bay / Windjammer / Sandals Grande / Sugar Beach / Jade Mountain-Anse Chastanet / Ladera / Harbor Club | Resort and hotel-restaurant demand cluster. ## Notes for the Unyra sales team

- Start with `fresh garlic` pages when the target is the core commodity market.
- Use `peeled garlic` pages for foodservice/convenience targeting.
- Treat generic `garlic` pages as broader lead lists that may include mixed garlic products.
- Before quoting, validate each target for exact product fit, current activity, certifications, and reachable decision-maker.
